| QA-358 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | CatchResolver.cs:249-263 | When only a defender is in reach (overthrown ball, receiver beaten), the result is 80% Deflect and ~20% Intercept regardless of whether the pass was catchable by the receiver; a ball thrown 3 yards over everyone's head near a safety turns into a tip that then flows into the tip drill (56% catch for offense), which is unrealistic. | Only allow a deflection when the ball is within the defender's reach and he faces it; otherwise let the ball fly. |  |
| QA-359 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | CatchResolver.cs:231 | Tipped-ball drill gives the offense a 28-68% catch chance on a ball just batted by a defender and defense only (0.06+0.30*skill)*0.7 (about 10-20%); balls deflected up in the air are caught by the offense far more often than by the defenders who tipped them. | Equalize and make it depend on who is closer and their facing. |  |
| QA-360 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | CatchResolver.cs:424-433 | CheckPitch only lets the intended receiver collect (d<1.15, Z 0.2-2.3) so pitches cannot be intercepted/knocked down by a defender who crosses the lane, and a mistimed pitch that passes a back at Z<0.2 or Z>2.3 is just a ground ball with no bobble animation. | Allow defenders to bat the pitch and add a bobble outcome. |  |
| QA-361 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | CatchResolver.cs:360-363 | InBoundsCatch tests the body centre against need (0.14 - 0.08*awareness yards in pro rules), so a receiver 0.14 yards inside the sideline with his body centre in and feet out still catches; feet are not modelled and a receiver sliding along the sideline gets the same radius as one cutting toward it. | Use the lead foot position (Pos + Facing*0.2) and Vel direction. |  |
| QA-362 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.cs:79 | _hotMode is set when PassRushers >= Math.Max(5, protectors - (cond ? 0 : 0)); the ternary is a no-op and the test ignores how many blockers exist, so any 5-man rush triggers a short drop and quick throws even against a 7-man protection, while 4 rushers vs 3 blockers (a real overload) is not hot. | Compare rushers against available blockers (hot when rushers > protectors) and remove the dead ternary. |  |
| QA-363 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.cs:109,121 + OffenseAI.Qb.cs:105 | OnSnap takes the snapper as Agents[4] and the ball starts at its Pos; the QB-under-center exchange is instantaneous (S.GiveBall(_qb) at the snap tick) so the ball teleports from the center's hands to the QB with no snap travel time or animation window. | Launch a short BallState.Snap even for under-center snaps (0.15 s) and give the ball on arrival. |  |
| QA-364 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.cs:449 | RunnerPreBall indexes ca.Path[ca.Path.Count - 1] for a fake carrier before the Path.Count==0 guard on the next lines; a CarryAssignment with IsFake and an empty path throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException and kills the play loop. | Move the Count==0 guard above the fake-carrier test. |  |
| QA-366 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.cs:269-277 | Waypoints advance when within 1.3 yards (0.85 on sharp corners), so a receiver cuts every route short by about a yard: a 10-yard out turns at ~8.7 and the stem is rounded, which makes slants/outs/hitches look lazy and gives the corner more room to undercut. | Advance on reaching the waypoint (<=0.4 yd) or use projection past the waypoint; keep the leash only for vertical stems. |  |
| QA-368 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.cs:235-240 | A jammed receiver (JamTime>0) runs straight up the field at 35% speed regardless of his route, ignoring the route clock and never advancing PathIdx, so after the jam ends he is on the wrong stem and the QB's EarliestThrow timing is off. | Keep the route running at reduced speed, stepping PathIdx along the projected path. |  |
| QA-369 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.cs:324-351 | FindSoftSpot shares a.ReactionClock and a.Landmark with the carrier/defender AI, scores spots by distance to the nearest defender only (no zone-hole or QB-lane awareness), and clamps Y to LosY-1, so a settling receiver can pick a spot behind the line or inside the pass-rush lane. | Use a dedicated field and add a QB-throw-lane/LOS clamp (>= LosY+1). |  |
| QA-370 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.cs:402-414 | TrackBall only searches the next 2 s (40 x 0.05) of the ball flight for a reachable point; deep throws with >2 s remaining and an overthrow leave the receiver chasing the nominal catchZ=1.6 point at the landing spot, so receivers run to where the ball lands and not to where they can reach it. | Search the whole flight (up to tf) in 0.05 s steps. |  |
| QA-371 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.cs:459-462 | Runner pre-ball speed is hard-coded (vmax*0.82, floor Math.Max(2.5, dist*5)) so the back never stops at the mesh point; a back who reaches his final waypoint keeps walking at 2.5 yd/s into the QB. | Hold position (Want zero) once within 0.5 yd of the last waypoint and the ball has not arrived. |  |
| QA-373 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:20-26 | The bad-snap branch is an empty block (// bad snap: scramble for it); after a fumbled snap the QB stays in WaitSnap forever, standing frozen facing downfield while the ball lies on the grass, and never dives on it. | On Ball.Fumbled during WaitSnap set the QB to chase the loose ball (Want toward Ball.Pos) and let the loose-ball logic recover it. |  |
| QA-374 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:215-231 | PocketDrift pushes the QB away from every rusher within 4.5 yards using the full 2D vector, so a rusher in front pushes the QB straight backwards (toward his own end zone and into safeties); the comment promises "step up when they have gone wide" but there is no forward component. Deep drift turns a sack into a safety. | Restrict the drift to lateral movement plus a step-up when edge rushers are beyond the QB; clamp Y >= LosY-9. |  |
| QA-376 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:92-99 | A Toss only pitches when S.Time>=0.28, 2<dist<7.5 and /tgt.Vel.X/>1.5; if the back is slow to the edge the toss never happens, the QB stays frozen (Want zero) for 1.1 s and then runs the ball himself as a keeper with the QbState Done. | Pitch at a deadline regardless of the runner's speed or hand off instead. |  |
| QA-377 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:120-127 | The handoff fires as soon as the QB and the back are within 1.6 yards and the ball teleports into the back's hands with no exchange animation window; combined with SeparateBodies ghosting (PlaySimulation.cs:469) the QB and back overlap during the mesh. | Add a 0.15 s exchange window with the ball interpolated between hands. |  |
| QA-378 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:272-280 | Threshold clamps at max(0.45, ...) so a pass is only thrown when the best receiver is rated >= 0.45 open; with the QB bailing to throwaway at holdMax (up to 3.3 s) if nobody reaches 0.45 the engine over-produces throwaways and under-produces forced throws into coverage, and every QB, regardless of rating, has the same floor. | Lower the floor for high-aggression QBs and let low-awareness QBs force throws. |  |
| QA-379 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:385 | Throw-away is chosen when aw>0.35 and (press>=0.50 or Time>holdMax+0.05); there is no check that the QB is in the pocket vs outside the tackle box nor of the down/field position (a throwaway on 4th down or in field-goal range). | Make the throwaway decision depend on down and distance. |  |
| QA-380 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:196 | EnterSet resets ReadIdx to 0 and ReadTimer; a rolling QB who has already worked through two reads on the move (HandleReads at :188) restarts the progression from the first read when he stops, wasting time. | Keep ReadIdx when entering Set from a roll. |  |
| QA-381 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:499-528 | Pass velocity is clamped to MaxThrowSpeed 22 + 12*rating01 (up to 34 yd/s = 70 mph) and `flat = 16 + d` clamps to that, so a 20-yard out leaves the hand at 34 yd/s and arrives in ~0.65 s (NFL ~1.0 s); defenders have no time to react and a 99 throw power QB throws 15 mph harder than any NFL QB. Lob style only appears at d>=58 (never, because tf>2.7 returns -1) so touch/loft passes over linebackers essentially never occur. | Cap speeds at 26-28 yd/s for elite arms, raise flight time, and choose Lob for d between 20 and 45 when a defender is in the lane. |  |
| QA-382 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:424 | EvalReceiver returns -1 for tf>2.7 s; with the speeds above this blocks balls over ~48-50 yards from ever being thrown except via HailMary, and the 3-iteration lead solve is style-fixed so deep throws rarely track a receiver who is running away. | Allow tf up to 3.4 s and add more iterations for deep routes. |  |
| QA-384 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:588-629 | ThrowAway ignores the pass-rusher: it targets sideX=-1.5 or Width+1.5 even if the QB is in the middle of the pocket and a DE is standing on that line, so the "throwaway" can be a deflection that goes to a defender, and the intentional-grounding flag is only raised 50% when no receiver is within 11 yards. | Pick the sideline away from the pass rush and raise the grounding probability. |  |
| QA-385 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:710-716 | A user pushing the stick >0.45 in Dropback after 0.7 s turns the QB into Scramble even if still 3 yards behind the LOS in a drop, killing the set and the progression reads; AI blockers still use pass-pro landmarks so the pocket collapses. | Require reaching the drop point or a bigger deadzone. |  |
| QA-386 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | CarrierAI.cs:27-32 | LaneFinder only considers headings within +-92 degrees of straight downfield, so a runner can never cut back more than 90 degrees; backside cutbacks and returners reversing field are impossible and the carrier "runs into" the pursuit instead of cutting back. | Allow +-150 degrees at low speed. |  |
| QA-387 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | CarrierAI.cs:124-133 | ReactionClock is shared with FindSoftSpot, DefenseAI and the throwing logic; a receiver who catches in the middle of FindSoftSpot's 0.2 s cache or an interceptor reusing coverage state reads a stale LastTargetDir for up to 0.2 s, producing a straight run for 12 frames. | Use a dedicated carrier clock and reset it on GiveBall. |  |
| QA-388 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | CarrierAI.cs:136-141 | AI carriers always run at 0.955 vmax and Exertion 0, so CPU backs and returners are never at top speed and never drain stamina, while user carriers hit 1.0 with sprint; the CPU ball carrier is 4.5% slower than the same player controlled by the user. | Use 1.0 when open field and set Exertion for fatigue. |  |
| QA-389 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | CarrierMoves.cs:62-65 | TryMove has a dead statement (if (c.Side == S.BallSide == false && !c.HasBall) { }) with a confusing chained comparison, and the user-defender path for Dive depends on it. | Remove it. |  |
| QA-390 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | CarrierMoves.cs:132-203 | The move contest is resolved the instant the move starts against the nearest defender within 2-2.8 yards, so pressing juke/spin early stumbles a defender who is still 2.8 yards away and grants 0.65 s of mutual tackle immunity, making the user moves much too effective when mashed; a failed move has no consequence for juke/stiff-arm. | Resolve when a defender actually reaches tackle range during the move window and add a failure penalty. |  |
| QA-391 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | CarrierMoves.cs:123,33-47 | Dive always ends the play with EndReason.Tackle when MoveTime expires (even at midfield with no defender within 20 yards) and the Tackle event is logged with AgentA=-1, which also yields TacklerId=-1 and breaks the stat/animation lookups. | Only end the play for a goal-line dive that reaches the end zone/slide; set a tackler or log a Slide event. |  |
| QA-392 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | CarrierMoves.cs:91 | Spin direction is random (Rng.NextFloat()<0.5) rather than away from the closest defender, so AI and user spins frequently turn into the tackler. | Spin away from the threat's lateral side. |  |
| QA-393 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | PhysicalGameSim.cs:170-173 | Pick() returns null for an empty list (kick returns, FG blocks, onsides); `off = _extraPoint`/_fieldGoal can also be null, so a trimmed custom playbook produces NullReferenceException at off.Name in PlayNext. | Fall back to the first play of the category and assert at Index(). |  |
| QA-394 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | PhysicalGameSim.cs:142-146 + SimRosters.cs:16-27 | ForFormation always picks the best available depth-chart player, so starters play every snap of every drive (including kick coverage and returns) with no substitution, no rest and fresh legs; combined with AgentBody Energy=1 each play, fatigue, rotation and depth never matter. | Add a rotation/fatigue model (energy persisted per player) and special-teams specialists. |  |
| QA-396 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | PhysicalGameSim.cs:199-204 | The CPU onside-kick decision requires Quarter==4 and ClockSeconds<200 and trailing by 1-8, so teams trailing by 9-16 with 2 minutes left never onside, and a trailing team in the final 30 seconds of the 2nd half uses a normal deep kick. | Use needed-possessions logic and time remaining. |  |
| QA-397 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockerAI.cs:219-228 | AssignRunBlocks computes a careful monotone (non-crossing) matching and stores it in BlockTargetId, but on the first UpdateBlocker tick ReactionClock is 0 so PickTarget runs and replaces it; the preassignment only survives as a +3.0 (zone) or +1.0 (man) bias, so blockers regularly pick the same man (TargetedByOthers only subtracts 1.1) and leave a defender unblocked at the snap. | Honor BlockTargetId for the first 0.3 s (initialize ReactionClock to 0.3 at OnSnap) and only retarget when the man is invalid. |  |
| QA-398 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockerAI.cs:340-350 | LeadMove: when the lead blocker is far from the hole and S.Time<0.6 the first branch sets Want toward the hole, but unless t==null it falls through and the next lines overwrite Want with the direct-to-target vector, so the "lead to the hole first" path never executes; the lead back/FB runs at his man across the formation instead of through the hole. | Add a return after the hole movement. |  |
| QA-400 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockerAI.cs:243-266 | Pass protection has no kick-slide or pocket shape: blockers are clamped to Y<=LosY+0.9 and just track the nearest rusher between him and the QB, never retreat to form a cup; an edge rusher with speed simply runs around the tackle's outside shoulder (ConsiderSlip is only 1-7%) while the OL stays square at the line. | Add a set point 1-2 yards behind the LOS and an outside-shoulder bias so tackles kick-slide. |  |
| QA-402 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockerAI.cs:394-424 | PassProTarget returns `current` whenever the old man is within 7 yards, even if he is already shed/behind the blocker or engaged by another lineman, and only picks new rushers when current is null; a blitzing LB who goes unblocked is never picked up because every OL stays loyal to a DL he can no longer reach. | Re-evaluate every 0.2 s and prefer the most dangerous unblocked rusher; switch if current is beaten and a free rusher exists. |  |
| QA-403 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockerAI.cs:317,18 | PullMove sets Body.SpeedMultiplier = 1.0 every tick, wiping any drag or injury slow-down (TackleResolver sets SpeedMultiplier for dragged carriers and Spin/Truck), and pullers use HasFace=false so they run facing sideways until they reach a wp. | Respect existing multipliers and face the path direction explicitly. |  |
| QA-404 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:69 | Dead code: `if (false && S.OffPlay.Category == PlayCategory.Draw ...) return true;` in PassMode; the draw is handled through a second hack in ApplyPairMotion (BlockEngagement.cs:543-551). | Remove the dead branch and keep a single draw implementation. |  |
| QA-405 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:176-181 | Engagement starts for any blocker/defender pair within radius sum + 0.42 (about 1.2 yd), picking the closest defender (assigned only -0.6), so at the snap the OL locks the DL directly across even if the assignment is a combo/pull or a different gap, and a puller (guarded by only PullStage<1 && within 1.5 yd) engages the first DL he brushes while pulling; there is no technique/step timing before contact. | Require a 0.1-0.2 s step before contact and respect the assigned target first. |  |
| QA-406 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:290-294 | The pass-protection drift constant bias -0.18 combined with a mean-reverting term 0.28*Lev puts the mean engagement lev at about -0.8 for evenly matched players, so evenly matched blocks are mostly "losing" (pocket pressure p>=0.35 whenever Lev<-0.35 per PocketPressure) from the first second; the QB feels pressure the entire time even when the line is dominating (gap>0.2 needed to hold lev>-0.2). | Set bias to ~0 for even ratings or compute pressure from distance, not Lev. |  |
| QA-407 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:292 | Lev random walk noise is 0.62*sqrt(dt) per step (sigma 0.62/s) with no rating dependency; against reversion 0.28 this is 0.83 stationary sigma, bigger than the entire +-1 range, so outcomes are dominated by noise: a 90-rated tackle vs a 60 rusher still gets beaten in many snaps, and a 99 pass rusher vs 40 OL isn't guaranteed to win. | Scale noise down (0.3) or use rating-based variance. |  |
| QA-408 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:309,319 | Holding probability: per tick scale*0.10*... while Lev<-0.45 and a flat 0.8*0.10*... on shed; with the awareness/passblocking multipliers the foul can only be called on the blocker[0], never the second blocker or a defender grabbing a receiver (DefensiveHolding appears once elsewhere), and not when the rusher is clear of the pocket. | Randomize which blocker and add defensive holding in coverage. |  |
| QA-409 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:383-387 | Illegal block in the back is rolled at a flat 7% for every engagement that begins on a defender 3+ yards past the LOS who is facing away, with no check on where the blocker is relative to the defender's numbers; many crack/stalk blocks on fleeing DBs are flagged. | Use the relative angle and the distance to the ball carrier. |  |
| QA-410 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:531-562 | ApplyPairMotion caps the pair velocity at 0.45*min(VMax) and uses a spring toward spacing s0, but the spring stiffness is 6/s with 0.5 weight each, so a pair drifting at 2.8 yd/s bursts through other bodies (SeparateBodies skips the engaged pair); a bull-rush pile can walk the QB 3 yards back in a second, which looks like OL being thrown backwards, not stand-up pass sets. | Reduce pmax in pass mode to ~1.2 yd/s and check collisions with other agents. |  |
| QA-411 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:315 | Shed duration = 0.14 + (1-BlockShedding01)*0.20 s so every shed completes in 0.14-0.34 s and the shed event is logged at e.Age without any animation lead time; the animator never gets a stage to show the defender swimming/ripping past. | Hold IsShedding for >=0.35 s with an explicit move animation tag. |  |
| QA-412 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:330 | A pass-mode pancake happens only when Lev>=1 for 1.4 s and then only 2% per tick, so lineman dominance never produces pancake animation in pass pro, while in run mode HighLevTime>0.18 s alone knocks the defender to the ground for 0.9-1.4 s on every winning block (pancake rate looks like 1 per 3-4 plays). | Make the run pancake probability depend on the lev margin and mass difference. |  |
| QA-413 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:346-358 | Combo-block peel is chosen by the nearest uncovered LB/S within 9 yards for either blocker, with no check that the climbing blocker's side faces the LB; peeled blockers get NoEngage 1.5 s on the original DL and reset ReactionClock so they re-pick the same DL half the time, producing flip-flopping doubles. | Choose the blocker on the playside and freeze target for 0.5 s. |  |
| QA-414 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:232 | MassScore normalizes (mass-80)/80 on kg, giving a 100 kg TE only 25 and a 150 kg OL 87.5 while carrying 15% weight; the cap at 160 kg means the 350 lb OL vs 300 lb DT difference is almost zero; heavy NT/OL pairs have no mass advantage beyond 160. | Raise the cap to 180 kg and use a non-linear weight. |  |
| QA-415 | open | S1 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:395,399,390-393 | In ManUpdate the target point is `rp + rv*0.25 - rdir*cushion`, i.e. a point BEHIND the receiver (toward the offense) by the cushion; for an off-man corner aligned 7 yards deep, startCushion is ~6.7 so the desired point is ~6.7 yards behind the WR (and 1.2 yards behind him at the line for press). The gain `to*3.6` then makes the CB sprint toward the QB side at the snap, run into the WR, be pushed aside by SeparateBodies and end up trailing; zone coverage uses +cushion (DefenseAI.cs:501) so the two disagree. Visible as CBs charging the line / running backwards-looking at the snap. Verify in play (off-man vs a vertical route). | Use `rp + rdir*cushion` (in front of the receiver) until he is beaten, then switch to a trail point; unit-test the first 0.5 s of off-man alignment. |  |
| QA-416 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:403,355,373 | Man defenders use the receiver's actual recent velocity as feed-forward (want = rv*0.97 + to*3.6, reaction only 0.07-0.25 s of position history), so a CB mirrors every break with a perfect read of the route; the only separation comes from the hip-freeze (0.09-0.4 s) and raw speed, so a WR of equal speed cannot get open deep and routes with a quick break are the only way to beat man. | Add a decision delay on the break (mirror the stem, not the future vector) and make the lag depend on ManCoverage and the receiver's route-running. |  |
| QA-417 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:71-112 | MatchMen assigns defender-to-receiver by a greedy sorted-pairs pass using X distance plus 0.25*Y; safeties have no position penalty so a FS can take a slot WR while a CB is left covering the TE/RB (cost +2.0), and matching is static from the snap, so crossing routes and switches/rubs are never handled (a defender trails his man through a pick). | Use a Hungarian/DP assignment by position class and add a simple switch rule when two man defenders' receivers cross. |  |
| QA-418 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:340-347 | A man defender whose receiver has a BlockAssignment (or whose RB is engaged) is silently converted to a HookMiddle zone defender at LosY+8 and then plays zone for the rest of the play, so a CB covering a TE/slot who stays in to block drops to the middle hook rather than doubling or blitzing; this also fires for fake carriers in play action. | Let him help on the nearest vertical route or blitz when the man blocks. |  |
| QA-420 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:414-428 | Illegal contact is rolled each tick (0.0095*(1.45-aw)*(1.3-man)) whenever a man defender is within 1.7 yards of a receiver more than 6 yards downfield between 1.1 s and 3.4 s, with no actual contact test; a normal trail technique at 1.5 yard cushion sits inside that window so the flag fires in roughly 25-45% of man-coverage pass plays per defender; DefensiveHolding when beaten adds more. NFL: ~0.1 illegal contact per game. | Gate on actual body contact (SeparateBodies overlap) and lower the rate by an order of magnitude. |  |
| QA-421 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:430-449 | PressJam is resolved once when the receiver gets within 1.9 yards after 0.12 s: a won jam only sets r.JamTime 0.30-0.65 s and halves velocity (OffenseAI makes the jammed WR run straight up the field at 35% speed), and a lost jam freezes the CB for 0.25-0.4 s with HeldWant (0,-2); there is no physical push, no hand-fight animation tag and the jam applies even to receivers already past the CB. | Check relative geometry (receiver in front of the defender) and apply a lateral displacement plus a proper Jam action. |  |
| QA-422 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:495-512 | Zone defenders re-choose their receiver from scratch every tick by -dist + relY*0.3 - 0.5/relX/ (+3 for the target), so two adjacent defenders often lock onto the same receiver and flip between receivers whenever the score crosses; no handoff of vertical routes between zones (no 'zone match') and receivers leaving a zone are dropped instantly. | Add hysteresis (keep the previous tgt unless a better one by >=1.5 score) and a coordinator that removes a receiver already taken by a neighbor. |  |
| QA-423 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:495 | Zone defenders ignore receivers for the first 0.45 s (desired = landmark), so a slot receiver releasing through a hook zone defender in the first half second is not jammed or re-routed and the LB stays at his landmark even if the receiver is on top of him. | Add a bump/read window for receivers within 2 yards from the snap. |  |
| QA-425 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:663-703 | PursueTarget points every defender at the same predicted intercept (aim = carrier + vpred*tInt*lead) with no leverage roles: the comment 'sideline defenders force the carrier inside: keep leverage' has no code. All 11 collapse on the carrier along identical angles which produces pile-ons, never a contain or cutback lane, and lets a runner outside the tackle find no one outside leverage. | Assign contain/force/backside roles by alignment and keep outside leverage until the carrier commits. |  |
| QA-426 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:705-723 | TryTackleDive rolls Rng each tick in the 1.5-2.7 yd window (1.0-2.2% per tick), so diving frequency depends on how long a defender sits in the window and consumes RNG at frame granularity; QB-chasing pass rushers dive about 10-35% of approaches. | Decide once per approach with a timer. |  |
| QA-427 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:254-285 | After 0.22 s every RushAssignment defender runs at full speed straight at the QB (or the carrier); there is no rush lane discipline (contain only for assigned contain men), no rush counter after being engaged except stunt aim for 0.35 s, and rushers do not 'peel off' to a screen or draw they do not recognize before 0.55 s (DrawHold). Screens (OffPlay.Category Screen) get no defensive recognition at all. | Add screen recognition (receiver turning around behind the LOS) to drop rushers into pursuit. |  |
| QA-428 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:262 | Blitzers/pass rushers aim at GapPoint = LosY-0.5 for the first 0.22 s, a point behind the line; players starting at 1 yard off the ball (LBs) therefore stop at the gap, and the Delay 0.55 s window for blitzers only applies to a.Pos.Y > LosY+0.5 so a blitzing LB who starts at 4 yards runs through the gap point at the line before heading to the QB (slow approach angle). | Aim at the final target through the gap with a smooth bend. |  |
| QA-429 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:208-211 | While a pass is in the air only defenders with BreakOnBall (covering with resp>=0.45) react; the other eligible defenders (DL, rushers) never turn to the ball, so linemen continue to the QB even when the pass is already thrown 5 yards away and batted-ball jumps come only from CatchResolver random Engages; no pass-rusher 'hands up' reaction. | Allow DL within 3 yards of the passing lane to jump and the others to pursue the ball. |  |
| QA-430 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:129-132 | ReadDelay: linebackers +0.15 s and DBs +0.20 s on top of 0.20+0.32*(1-aw), so a 70-awareness safety takes 0.5-0.6 s to react to a handoff and 0.8 s for low rated; combined with misdirection delays (0.52 s counter) the secondary never supports against the run until the RB is 5+ yards downfield. | Reduce the base to 0.12 and add the DB penalty only for play-action. |  |
| QA-432 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:166-167,170-171 | The defense skips agents in State Tackling entirely (no Want) so a defender who whiffs (BrokenTackle/Shoestring set Tackling) and lies on the ground then stands up in Normal after 0.75 s (TackleResolver.cs:32) while his FallenTime may be up to 1.3 s; he can be Normal and still Fallen for 0.5 s (Want ignored by Body.Step) and the pursuit AI state reset is delayed. | Tie Tackling recovery to FallenTime reaching zero. |  |
| QA-433 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:300-312 | BlitzUpdate: before the Delay expires the defender is frozen with Want=0 and FaceDir (0,-1); for show-blitz pre-snap alignment (y<=1.2) a blitzer with Delay>0 stands still for up to 0.5 s after the snap right behind the DL while the OL is already engaging the line, then he runs into his own teammates. | Move the blitzer to the aim point during the delay or have him back off. |  |
| QA-434 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:315-330 | SpyUpdate holds at Y=LosY+5 at the QB's X and faces the QB; the spy never reacts to the QB crossing less than 3 yds/s and has no mirror tolerance, so a QB sliding out of the pocket slower than 3 yd/s (the common scramble start) is not spied. | Use the QB's lateral direction and mirror at any speed. |  |
| QA-435 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:736 | UserDefenderInput calls BallReaction(a) every tick when the stick is neutral during a pass in flight, bypassing the BreakOnBall/BallBreakAt reaction window, so a user DB with zero input reacts instantly with perfect ball tracking while AI defenders wait their reaction time. | Respect BallBreakAt for the user as well or only auto-assist inside a radius. |  |
| QA-436 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:731-734 | UserDefenderInput maps both Dive and Tackle to a dive tackle; there is no standing tackle/ wrap-up button and the auto-tackle only triggers at reach 1.28 yd, so a user defender cannot initiate a tackle attempt within range and cannot shoulder-charge. | Add a tackle attempt that forces TackleResolver.Attempt at a longer reach. |  |
| QA-437 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:782-793 | Responsibility returns 1.0 for any RushAssignment/BlitzAssignment regardless of position, so EvalReceiver treats a DT 8 yards from the receiver as a full lane threat (via threat*=1 and the lane check), reducing 'open' for receivers that a DL cannot reach and pushing the QB toward throwaways. | Return a small value (0.1) for rushers beyond 3 yards of the catch point. |  |
| QA-438 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:59-61,183-184 | PerceivesRun/QbDrawRunning treat a QB past LosY+0.5 as running; a QB on a draw is detected only after t>_exchangeAt (0.85 s) so linebackers drop 0.55-0.95 s into the draw hold and then take another 0.02-0.17 s; QB draws and sneaks gain big yardage easily. | Reduce DrawHold for QB keeps and trigger from the QB's heading change. |  |
| QA-440 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:115,215-219 | On kickoffs every LaneAssignment man of the kicking team runs at 0.92 vmax from t=0 while the kick happens at 1.05 s (or later), so the coverage unit is already ~7 yards past the ball when it is kicked (offside in the NFL) and arrives at the return wall before the ball lands; there is no restraint line, no kicker-timed release and no offside. | Hold the coverage at the line until the kicker's last step (zero speed until the kick, or release at 0.8 s behind the ball). |  |
| QA-441 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:227-228 | If the kicker is not within 1.6 yards of the tee by _kickTime the kick still happens 0.6 s later, and the ball is created at (BallX0, LosY, 0.3) regardless of the kicker's position, so the ball teleports to the tee from wherever the kicker is. | Delay the kick until the kicker reaches the tee, or spawn the ball at the kicker's foot. |  |
| QA-442 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:231-241 | KickerRunUp is a straight line to the tee at min(0.7 vmax, 5+4t) and Want is not reduced near the ball, so the kicker runs through the ball spot and overlaps it; there is no curved approach or plant step. | Slow to a stop 1.2 yards before the tee and curve in from the side. |  |
| QA-443 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:664 | Fair catch is decided by a deterministic distance threshold: the returner fair-catches if ANY kicking-team player is within 16.5 (+4*(1-aw)) yards (or 8 yards when the call forbids it), but gunners are always within 10-15 yards at the catch, so nearly every punt is fair caught and returns are rare; no random/rating component and no signal animation. | Use a probability from distance to the nearest gunner, the returner's awareness and score/field position. |  |
| QA-444 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:718-730,751-760 | Bouncing-kick recovery rolls a probability on EVERY tick (60 Hz) while a player is within reach, so the 0.55-0.9 receiving catch chance and 0.35-0.75 onside recovery chance are effectively 100% after a few frames; muffs of bouncing kicks and onside outcomes depend only on who arrives first. Downing a punt (0.5 per tick) also happens almost immediately. | Roll once per player per touch (lock for 0.4 s after a failed attempt). |  |
| QA-445 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:709-715 | The receiving-team recovery loop only requires `a.State != Down/Engaged` and distance, so a blocking return man or even the QB-slot agent can field a bouncing kick, and a kickoff recovered by the kicking team requires `far` >= 9.5 yards but not that the receiving team had a chance to touch it first (10-yard rule is not enforced; an onside kick that travels 10 yd then bounces is recovered with the same logic). | Add the 10-yard touch rule with a short first-touch window for the receiving team. |  |
| QA-446 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:419-423 | DoPunt blocks the kick with probability 0.55 + 0.3*jump - 0.2*awareness whenever ANY defender is within 1.35 yards of the punter's release point; a rusher that gets within that distance almost always blocks (55-85%), so if one rusher beats his protector the punt is blocked (NFL block rate ~1-2% of punts). | Use a smaller per-rusher probability (about 0.15-0.25) and require the rusher to be in front of the punter at the kick. |  |
| QA-447 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:361 | Punt gunners (LaneAssignment) stand still (Stance) until S.Time>=0.35 although the snap occurs at t=0; the punt flight takes 0.8-1.2 s from snap so gunners are released ~0.35 s late and a punt is rarely downed inside the 10; real gunners release on the snap. | Release at the snap (after a 0.1 s jam). |  |
| QA-448 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:589-601 | UpdateKickBall only lets agents with ReturnerAssignment field a kicked ball in flight; if the returner is injured/down or the only returner is slot-mismatched, the ball is simply left to bounce and is touched by anybody on the loose-kick logic; no 'jam the returner' or a second returner moves up to field a short kick (backup returner holds spot: ReturnerUpdate L314-318). | Let the backup returner or nearest receiving player attempt the catch for squibs/short kicks. |  |
| QA-449 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:643-655 | A kickoff/punt caught in the end zone is only downed (touchback) when y-100 > 3.2 (or always for punts); between 100 and 103.2 the returner is forced to run it out, even for a short 1-yard-deep catch with the coverage 30 yards away. | Let the AI choose based on distance to the nearest coverage man and the return ability. |  |
| QA-450 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:627 | AttemptCatch muff probability 0.90 + 0.09*skill - 0.04*speedFrac (+ -0.04 for high/low balls) gives 3-10% muffs per kick for an 80-rated returner: roughly 1 muff in 15 kicks vs ~1 in 60 in the NFL; combined with the per-tick recovery the muffed ball is then recovered immediately by the returner's team. | Raise base to 0.97. |  |
| QA-453 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:699-706 | The touchback rule for loose kicks triggers for any ball at Y>=100 that touches the ground or comes to rest, without checking whether the ball first landed in the field of play and rolled in (which is still a touchback) or whether the receiving team touched it; fine for deep kicks but a punt that lands at Y=99 and rolls into the end zone while a gunner is within reach is a touchback only after the gunner's per-tick downing chance fails. | Resolve downing vs touchback by who touches first. |  |
| QA-454 | open | S1 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:176-177 + DefaultPlaybooks.Defense.cs:366-385 | Contact range is radiusSum+0.42 (about 1.3 yd for heavy linemen) while interior DL line up 1.0-1.2 yards off the ball on the OL's Y=0; so every OL/DL pair across from each other is already inside contact range on the FIRST live tick and DetectNewEngagements locks them at the snap with zero steps, no first-step advantage and no slip (slip requires speed>3.5). Interior rushers can never penetrate a gap or run a twist; only edge rushers start free. | Reduce contact range (radius sum + 0.1) or add a 0.1-0.2 s engagement delay measured from the snap; let DL fire into gaps before locking. |  |
| QA-457 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | TackleResolver.cs:38-39 + PlaySimulation.Ball.cs:70-82 | After the pass is released the QB no longer holds the ball, so Tackles.Update (which only tests the ball carrier) never tackles him; pass rushers run through the QB and SeparateBodies shoves them, and the RoughingThePasser branch (TackleResolver.cs:262-266, qb && S.PassThrown) can never fire because the carrier is null. No late hits, no hit-as-he-throws, no QB knockdown after release. | Allow a defender to hit the passer within ~0.5 s of the release (event QbHit, optional RoughingThePasser flag) without ending the play. |  |
| QA-458 | open | S2 | ANI | Engine/AI | PlaySimulation.cs:496-501 | ResolveAction maps a standing agent to Action=Idle whenever Intent != Stance (the AI sets Intent=Run/Route/Idle on the first live tick) and Speed<0.5, so at the snap every DL, LB, DB and receiver switches Stance -> Idle (upright) for the 5-10 frames it takes to reach 0.5 yd/s, then Run; players visibly stand up before exploding off the line (OL are mapped to Block by L496 which is fine). | While StateTime/ActionTime < ~0.25 s after the snap, keep AgentAction.Stance (or a Burst/Start action) for agents whose speed is below 2 yd/s. |  |
| QA-464 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.cs:39-44, OffenseAI.cs:106-111 | If a scrimmage play's slot-0 assignment is not a QbAssignment (a wildcat/direct snap custom play or a malformed edited play), Prepare sets _qa=null and OnSnap returns before the snap, so the ball never leaves Dead state: nobody ever gets it and the play runs to the 22 s cap with TimeLimit. | Fall back to snapping to the first CarryAssignment holder, or validate in Setup. |  |
| QA-465 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | PlaySimulation.cs:640-677 | FalseStart, Offside, Encroachment and IllegalFormation are pure dice rolls (0.2-1.9%) that do not look at the real alignment (no check for fewer than 7 on the line, a DL beyond the ball, a man in motion towards the line, or defenders in the neutral zone), so you can never get an offside from a Press/Show-Blitz adjustment that places a player at y<=1.0 nor an illegal formation from a user-edited set. | Derive these fouls from LayoutOffense/LayoutDefense geometry and keep the dice only for jumps. |  |
| QA-466 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | PlaySimulation.PreSnap.cs:106-125 | Motion has no defensive response: man defenders only re-evaluate after the snap (ManUpdate), zone/LB alignments do not shift, and with AutoSnap=true the snap comes 0.6 s after setup so a motion man is snapped before he has travelled 2 yards. | Add a pre-snap defensive reaction to motion (CB follows in man, LB/S rotate in zone). |  |
| QA-467 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | PlaySimulation.cs:568-583 | SwitchUserPlayer picks the same-side agent nearest the ball/carrier, including OL and QBs, for both offense and defense, with no preference for eligible players; after a completed pass the first switch lands on a lineman. | Rank by role (ball carrier, receivers, nearest defender at the pass target) before distance. |  |
| QA-468 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | TackleResolver.cs:76,43-63 | A whiff only sets TackleCooldown 0.32 s; every defender within reach of the carrier re-attempts each time the cooldown expires, so three defenders in range produce a stream of tackle attempts and the carrier is statistically brought down within ~1 s even when each attempt has a 15% failure; broken-tackle chance compounds the other way (must beat every attempt). | Give each attempt a defender-specific lockout after a miss (1.0-1.5 s) and cap simultaneous attempts. |  |
| QA-469 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.cs:456-462 | RunnerPreBall keeps `Math.Max(2.5, dist*5)` as the minimum speed for all pre-handoff runners so a back who stops at the mesh point shoots past the QB; the shotgun speed cap (1.6 / 3.4 yd/s) is only active while the QB state is WaitSnap/Mesh and within 7.5 yards, after which a draw RB gets to full speed before the handoff. | Use the mesh timing rather than distance and cap speed until the exchange. |  |
| QA-470 | open | S2 | ANI | Engine/AI | PlaySimulation.cs:372-386,416 | WindDown (the Dead phase) never decrements OverrideLeft (only LiveStep does, line 416), so any one-shot action started shortly before the whistle (tackle wrap-up 0.8 s, Throw 0.5 s, Catch 0.35 s, Handoff, Kick, Jump) stays frozen as the agent's Action for the whole post-play period and tacklers/receivers/QBs hold that pose until the next play. | Decrement OverrideLeft in WindDown (and in the pre-snap step). |  |
| QA-471 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | TackleResolver.cs:174 | The push-out-of-bounds test has `d.R.Tackling < 92`: elite tacklers (>=92) can never push a runner out, while the worst tacklers can; the condition looks inverted (strong tacklers should be better at driving a man out) and makes sideline runs easier against the best defenders. | Use a probability that grows with Tackling/Strength. |  |
| QA-472 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockerAI.cs:438-462 | RunDir() falls back to +1 (right) for any play without a CarryAssignment (QB keeper, option keep, scramble after a pass play, kneel), so all zone blockers shade right on a left-side scramble and the blocking target score favors the wrong side. | Derive the direction from the QB's heading or rollout direction. |  |
| QA-473 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | PlaySimulation.PreSnap.cs:166-180 | Show-blitz moves LBs/safeties to y<=1.2 (and CBs to 1.6) but never re-spaces them, so a show-blitzing MLB at x=0 sits 1.2 yards from the center and is inside engagement range at the snap; he is locked by the C without rushing and the blitz 'delay' logic never matters. | After shifting y re-spread blitzers to the gaps (A/B gap X) and assign the blitz gap. |  |
| QA-474 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:92-99 | The QB toss pitches from the QB's own position (PitchBall at PlaySimulation.Ball.cs:55 uses from.Pos) at z=1.2 with 15 yd/s min speed and the pitch is not preceded by a QB reverse-pivot; the QB has Want=Vec2.Zero during the mesh so a toss is thrown from a standing QB at the snap spot, not a quick step to the edge. | Let the QB step toward the runner (0.2 s) before the pitch. |  |
| QA-475 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:174-189 | QbDropback ends when the drop point is within 0.55 yards or after 3.2 s; for quick drops the shotgun depth equals the drop point so the QB is set instantly, but under-center 3-step drops are fast (v=0.92 vmax backpedal factor 0.5-0.64 => about 6 yd/s) so the QB covers 4.2 yards in ~0.8 s; with only 0.22 s read delay the QB sets and throws about 1.2 s after the snap, quicker than any real NFL 3-step (~1.7 s). | Slow the backpedal for drop steps or add a footwork timer. |  |
| QA-476 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | PlaySimulation.cs:554-565 | A user-controlled AI-less player who has no assignment (e.g. user picks a TE who is blocking) is treated as a free runner: ApplyUserControl sets Want from stick and Intent=Run, overriding Blocks.UpdateBlocker; the engaged lineman cannot be moved except by UserHint, but a non-engaged blocker walks away from his assignment and the AI does not backfill his block. | Give the user's blocker a pass-pro/run-block assist or reassign his man. |  |
| QA-477 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | Core/RuleSet.cs:29 | Pro RuleSet defaults FirstDownStopWindowSeconds=120, so inside the last 2:00 of Q2/Q4 every pro first down stops the clock (verified: a 12-yd gain at 1:40 leaves ClockRunning=false and a 20s restart runoff). The NFL clock keeps running after a first down, so two-minute drives get free stoppages and clock management is wrong. | Default to 0 for Pro (stop only for out of bounds, incompletions, timeouts, penalties) and keep the window only for the college variant. |  |
| QA-478 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | Core/RuleSet.cs:100 | College sets ClockStopsOnFirstDown=true for the entire game (pre-2023 NCAA behavior); current NCAA rules stop the clock on a first down only in the last 2 minutes of each half, so college games get too many snaps. | Use FirstDownStopWindowSeconds=120 for College instead of the blanket flag (or make it a rules toggle). |  |
| QA-479 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | Core/GameState.cs:58 | OvertimeReceiverDefault is hard-coded to Team.Home and the live MatchFlow never sets it (only QuickSim.cs:374 randomizes it), so in every played match the home team receives the OT kickoff with no coin toss, and AutoStartOvertime skips any choice. | Run an OT coin toss in MatchDirector (winner chooses) and pass the receiver to StartOvertime. |  |
| QA-480 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | Core/GameState.cs:428 | College OT: a defensive TD (interception or fumble return) on the FIRST possession calls CollegeOvertimeEnd(), which starts the second possession instead of ending the game (verified: tied game, pick-six makes it 10-16 and the other team still gets the ball at the 25). | When the defense scores and leads after any OT possession, call Finish() immediately. |  |
| QA-481 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | Core/GameState.cs:433 | College OT: a second-possession touchdown that already wins the game still goes to GamePhase.PointAfter, forcing a pointless PAT/two-point try before the game ends (lines 433-436 always enter PointAfter for the possession team). | If the scoring team leads after its TD and the opponent has no possession left, Finish() before the try. |  |
| QA-482 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | Core/GameState.cs:321 | ApplyPunt computes land=BallSpot+Yards with no blocked-punt handling: a negative Yards (verified -6 from own 3) yields spot>=100 and a receiver TD, and a kicking-team recovery behind the goal line can never be a safety. | Add Blocked/recovered-by flags and score safety or TD depending on who falls on it in which end zone. |  |
| QA-483 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | Core/GameState.cs:314 | A punt or kickoff RecoveredByKickingTeam clamps the spot to 1..99, so a muffed punt or onside kick that the kicking team recovers in the receiver's end zone is never a touchdown. | If land plus return reaches 100, ScoreTouchdown(kicker). |  |
| QA-484 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | Core/GameState.cs:186 | No 10-second runoff rule: an accepted offensive foul in the last minute only calls StopClock(), and dead-ball fouls zero PendingRunoff (ApplyPenaltyCore), so the offense can false-start at the end of a half or game with no clock penalty. | Implement the 10-second runoff for offensive fouls inside the final minute when the clock was running. |  |
| QA-485 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | Core/PlayCaller.cs:77 | ShouldKneel has no field-position check: at the offense's own 1-yard line the CPU kneels (verified), GameState turns end<=0 into a safety and the CPU hands the opponent 2 points while protecting a lead. | Return false when BallSpot<4 (QuickSim.ShouldKneel already does) or when a kneel would reach the end zone. |  |
| QA-486 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | Core/PenaltyEnforcer.cs:196 | EnforceOnTry sets HalfDistance=true on every try penalty and moves GameState.BallSpot from the PAT spot (85) even for a two-point try, while PlaySimulation.cs:167-168 resets LosY to Rules.PatSpot or TwoPointSpot every snap, so penalties on tries never change the replay spot. | Track try type, enforce from PatSpot/TwoPointSpot and let the sim read the enforced spot; set HalfDistance only when capped. |  |
| QA-487 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | Core/Penalty.cs:85 | College DPI: RuleSet.DpiSpotFoul=false (DpiMaxYards=15) but YardsFor still uses the spot of the foul capped at 15, so a 6-yard downfield college DPI moves the ball only 6 yards (verified identical to pro) instead of the flat 15 yards plus first down. | When !DpiSpotFoul return rules.DpiMaxYards regardless of FoulSpotYards. |  |
| QA-488 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | Core/Penalty.cs:87 | Math.Round on the foul spot (DPI at line 87, intentional grounding at line 93) uses banker's rounding, so a foul at 2.5 yards costs 2 but at 3.5 costs 4. | Use MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero or truncate consistently. |  |
| QA-489 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | Core/PenaltyEnforcer.cs:168 | PenaltyEnforcer.Offset (offsetting fouls) is never called and PlayResult carries a single PenaltyCall, so offsetting penalties and double fouls can never happen in the live game or QuickSim. | Allow two PenaltyCalls on a PlayResult and call Offset in GameState.Apply when both teams foul (replay the down). |  |
| QA-490 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | Core/PenaltyEnforcer.cs:90 | Kick-play fouls are not modeled: Enforce only handles Phase.Kickoff and Phase.PointAfter, so a foul on a punt or field goal (Phase Scrimmage) is enforced as an ordinary scrimmage foul with replay-the-down semantics; no roughing/running into the kicker (auto first down), no block-in-back from the end of a return, no play-counts fouls. | Add kick-play foul handling and the missing foul types. |  |
| QA-491 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | Core/Penalty.cs:44 | Penalty catalog has only 16 fouls; missing illegal shift/motion, ineligible receiver downfield, unsportsmanlike conduct, horse-collar, roughing the kicker, clipping/chop block, illegal substitution, so flags look repetitive (holding, false start, DPI). | Add the missing fouls with yardage and use them in QuickSim AttachPenalty and the live officials. |  |
| QA-493 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | Core/Penalty.cs:125 | PenaltyCall.For derives the offender from Penalty.Side and current Possession; in Phase.Kickoff Possession is the kicker, so Offside/Encroachment (Side=Defense) get blamed on the RECEIVING team although the kicking team is the one that jumps offside on a kickoff. | Special-case Phase.Kickoff (offside is the kicking team) or force callers to pass the team explicitly. |  |
| QA-494 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | Core/PenaltyEnforcer.cs:254 | ShouldAccept values states with ExpectedPoints (6*f^1.5-0.5, linear down/distance deductions) and ignores clock, timeouts and field-goal range, so late-game decisions (decline a penalty that stops the clock, accept a first-down penalty to kill clock) are mispriced for the CPU. | Add time/score awareness to Value() (win probability) or special-case the final two minutes. |  |
| QA-496 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | Core/PlayCaller.cs:66 | ShouldSpike never checks that the clock is actually running: verified after an incompletion at 0:25 with no timeouts it still returns true, so the CPU spikes on 2nd down when the clock is already stopped and burns a down. | Require s.ClockRunning and s.PendingRunoff>0 before spiking. |  |
| QA-497 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | Core/PlayCaller.cs:44 | The CPU/Ask-Coach offense pool explicitly excludes Hail Mary and nothing else throws one, so the CPU never attempts a Hail Mary as time expires in a half or game even when trailing from midfield. | Add a situational Hail Mary call (final snap of a half or game, ball between own 40 and opponent 45). |  |
| QA-501 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | Core/PlayCaller.cs:62 | CPU hurry-up in PlayCaller starts at 70s in Q2 and 150s in Q4 while QuickSim.DoScrimmage (QuickSimPlays.cs:457) hurries from 120s in Q2 and 300-480s in Q4, so the live CPU and the simulated CPU manage the clock differently and the live CPU runs the ball at 1:50. | Share one hurry-up helper between PlayCaller and QuickSim. |  |
| QA-502 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | Core/PlayCaller.cs:276 | FourthDown hard-codes field-goal range (toGoal+17 <= 54 pro, 50 college) regardless of the kicker or weather, so a weak kicker attempts 54-yard FGs and an elite kicker punts from the opponent 38; QuickSim uses a make-probability model instead. | Use the kicker's make probability (like QuickSim.FgMakeProb) to decide range. |  |
| QA-503 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | Core/PlayCaller.cs:359 | SuggestDefense returns hard-coded play names and takes the first play with that name (always the 4-3 Base version, file order), ignoring the defensive formation that matches the offense's personnel; a missing name silently shortens the list. | Filter by the chosen formation and fall back by category. |  |
| QA-505 | open | S2 | UI | Engine/AI | Core/PlayCallModel.cs:357 | Expire() can return null (Chosen=Displayed(null)): verified for a Side.Special model with no SpecialPlays it returns NULL, and any empty view with no coach picks or formations does the same, so the play-clock timeout hands a null play to the director. | Fall back to the first play of the book for that side (and never return null). |  |
| QA-535 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | Core/AudibleBook.cs:110 | DefenseAdjust.FindCoverage returns null when the formation lacks the requested shell (Dime has no Cover 2/3/0, 3-4 Base has no Cover 0/4, Goal Line only Cover 0/1/2), so the coverage audible silently does nothing. | Fall back to the closest scheme or disable unavailable choices in the UI. |  |
| QA-536 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | Core/AudibleBook.cs:55 | Default audible favourites always name the Right variants (Inside Zone Right, Outside Zone Right, Toss Sweep Right, Draw Right), so audibling from a Left run changes direction; only the Mirrored flag is carried over (line 76). | Prefer the variant matching the current play's Left/Right suffix. |  |
| QA-538 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | Core/PlayEditing.cs:218 | WithHotRoute lets any eligible receiver take a hot route on a run play and inserts him into the QB ReadProgression of a Handoff play, and ReadOrder on other routes is not renumbered, so ReadOrder and ReadProgression disagree. | Reject hot routes on run plays and renumber ReadOrder. |  |
| QA-553 | open | S3 | GFX | Rules | Core/Look/WeatherPresets.cs:132 | Random weather is 14 percent rain, 10 percent snow, 6 percent fog for every stadium and date with no climate or dome weighting, and time of day is independent of kickoff time, so a September game in Miami can snow. | Weight by stadium climate/season and add dome/roof handling. |  |
| QA-561 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | League/Offseason.cs:99 | Dead cap never accrues: Team.DeadCap is reset to 0 each offseason (and never incremented anywhere), and Release() discards the contract, so cutting a 4-year guaranteed deal is free and signing bonuses/guarantees are cosmetic (verified max DeadCap 0 over 8 seasons). | Add remaining prorated bonus plus guarantees to DeadCap when releasing or trading. |  |
| QA-562 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | League/Offseason.cs:449 | EnforceCap "restructures" by multiplying every contract above 3M by 0.94 repeatedly (115-574 restructures per offseason), silently cutting salaries across the roster until the team fits; stars are paid less every year instead of releasing or restructuring a few deals. | Restructure only the largest contracts (convert base to bonus) or release players. |  |
| QA-563 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | League/Season.cs:225 | HealWeekly runs before the week's games (line 200), so a freshly injured player is healed a week early: a 1-week injury misses zero games and a 2-week injury misses one; with the mean duration of about 3.6 weeks, roughly a third of injuries are meaningless. | Heal after the games (skip players injured this week). |  |
| QA-565 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/Awards.cs:148 | Rookie of the Year is never awarded after the first season: it requires p.DraftYear == s.Year, but Offseason.Run drafts with DraftYear = the season that just ended and then increments the year, so next-season rookies have DraftYear = Year-1 (verified RoyId=0 from season 2 on; season 1 awards a fake rookie from the generated league). | Compare DraftYear == s.Year-1 (or set the draft year to the upcoming season). |  |
| QA-566 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/Awards.cs:144 | MVP is OffenseValue plus 4 per team win and OPOY is OffenseValue, so MVP equals OPOY nearly every year (6 of 6 verified) and defenders/kickers can never win MVP. | Weight MVP by team success/position or exclude the OPOY winner. |  |
| QA-568 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | League/ScheduleGenerator.cs:136 | AssignProWeeks places games by random perfect matching with no constraints: verified streaks of 6-8 consecutive home or away games, 0-2 back-to-back division rematches per season, and week 18 with only 2-6 of 16 division games (the NFL plays all division games in the finale). | Add constraints (max 3 consecutive home/away, no back-to-back rematches, divisional finale). |  |
| QA-569 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | League/ScheduleGenerator.cs:99 | 17th-game host is decided by (i+y)%2 per Liberty division, so all four teams of 2 Liberty divisions host that game every year and the paired Frontier teams always go on the road (16 teams at 8 home games, 16 at 9), instead of alternating by team/year. | Alternate host by team and season. |  |
| QA-570 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | League/ScheduleGenerator.cs:222 | College conference schedules re-shuffle membership every season (rng.Shuffle(members)), so there are no fixed rivals or divisions; odd-sized conferences (13, 17) play only 8 conference games while even ones play 9; round-robin conferences (<=10) get random home/away (a team can host 2 of 7). | Seed pairings from the prior year/rivalry table and balance home games. |  |
| QA-571 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | League/ScheduleGenerator.cs:314 | AssignCollegeWeeks is greedy: verified 15 regular weeks with 28 games in week 14 and 4 in week 15 (tail teams play two weeks after everyone else), 4 teams with no bye in their span, and any game unplaced after 20 weeks keeps Week 0. | Schedule weeks with matching like the pro generator and guarantee byes. |  |
| QA-572 | open | S2 | SIM | Rules | League/Standings.cs:159 | BreakTie is a single comparator chain (head-to-head only if every pair met, then division %, conference %, point differential): no common-games, strength of victory or strength of schedule and no re-evaluation after eliminating teams, so multi-team ties and wild-card seeding are decided by point differential too early. | Implement the full NFL tiebreak procedure (or at least SOV/SOS before point differential). |  |
| QA-573 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | League/Season.cs:302 | DraftOrder ranks playoff teams by number of playoff wins only, so a divisional-round loser that had a bye (0 wins) ties a wild-card loser and is ordered by record, and the order ignores strength of schedule tiebreaks. | Order by elimination round, then record/strength of schedule. |  |
| QA-574 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | League/SeasonCollege.cs:231 | The bowl field loop lowers minWins down to 0 to fill 35 bowls, so teams below .500 get bowl bids when eligible teams run short (verified 6 team slots with fewer than 6 wins), and pairings are by committee rank with no conference tie-ins. | Stop at 5 wins and leave bowls unfilled or use conference tie-ins. |  |
| QA-580 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/Draft.cs:79 | Standalone (no college world) draft potential tops out near 86 and decline outpaces growth, so stars are never replaced: verified players rated >=90 fall 23 to 11 to 10 and >=95 fall 7 to 2 over seven seasons, QB top AAV drops from 32.7M to 24M, and the league flattens to team overalls 73-79. | Let top prospects reach 95+ potential and soften star decline. |  |
| QA-591 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSimPlays.cs:56 | Every accepted penalty is charged to a random OL (offense) or DL (defense) player regardless of type: DPI, defensive holding and illegal contact land on defensive linemen and OPI on offensive linemen (verified over a full season: 0 penalties for any WR, CB, S, LB or QB; DTs average 9 per season). | Choose the culprit by penalty type (CB/S for DPI, WR for OPI, OL for holding/false start, DL for offside). |  |
| QA-592 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSimPlays.cs:608 | The punt touchback branch returns before `o.Dist = dist` (line 625), so touchback punts record 0 yards in the box score and player punt yards and the play log reads "punt 0 yds". | Set o.Dist = dist before the early return. |  |
| QA-593 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSimPlays.cs:593 | QuickSim measures punts from the line of scrimmage (mean 44, box-score average 41.3) although real punters kick from about 14 yards behind it, so net field position after a punt is roughly 10 yards too deep versus the real 47-yard gross average. | Subtract the punter's depth (about 14) or lower the mean to about 33 from the LOS. |  |
| QA-594 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSimPlays.cs:524 | 4th-down logic treats `desperate` only when q==4, so in the second pro-OT possession a team trailing by a field goal punts on 4th-and-long instead of going for it; hurry/milk logic is also off in overtime. | Include OT stage 1 trailing situations in desperate. |  |
| QA-595 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | League/QuickSimPlays.cs:448 | Penalties are generated only on scrimmage run/pass plays (and disabled in college overtime): no flags on kickoffs, punts or field goals, no roughing/running into the kicker; the condition (!IsOvertime OR !IsCollegeOvertime) simplifies to `!IsCollegeOvertime`. | Add special-teams flags and allow penalties in college OT. |  |
| QA-596 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSimPlays.cs:384 | PAT success is 0.945 for both rule sets (`CollegeStyle ? 0.945f : 0.945f`) and the kicker term is 0.0016 per rating point on Compress-flattened ratings, so college XP (20-yard kick) is no easier and kicker quality barely matters. | Use about 0.965 for college and scale with kicker rating. |  |
| QA-598 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSimPlays.cs:231 | Punt returns count out-of-bounds punts as 0-yard return attempts (the condition excludes only FairCatch and Touchback), inflating PR attempts and lowering average. | Also exclude R.OutOfBounds. |  |
| QA-600 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | League/QuickSimPlays.cs:54 | Team and player PenaltyYds add the base YardsFor value, not the enforced yards (half-the-distance caps, spot-foul DPI), so penalty yardage totals can exceed real ball movement near the goal line. | Sum EnforcementResult.Yards from the actual enforcement. |  |
| QA-601 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSimPlays.cs:288 | Every tackle goes to a single solo tackler chosen from DL/LB/S weights (CBs never tackle runs) with no assists or sack-assist credit and TFL only on runs, so MLB tackles run high (average 135, top 181) and CB/DL tackles low. | Add assisted tackles and include CBs/safeties on runs and screens. |  |
| QA-602 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSimPassRun.cs:76 | Throw-type weights are never clamped after adjustments: in short yardage with weak WR Open or QB power (ratings near 40, e.g. dummy replacements) tp[3] goes slightly negative and Pick() skews the other bins. | Clamp each weight to >=0 before Pick. |  |
| QA-603 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSimPassRun.cs:127 | Completion probability has no field-position term and air yards are capped at the distance to the goal (lines 127/178), so any completion inside the 5 is a touchdown and the red-zone completion rate equals midfield (about 63 percent versus about 50 real). | Subtract completion probability inside the 20 and shrink air yards. |  |
| QA-604 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSim.cs:289 | The kick and punt returner is always WR4 (u.KR = u.WR[3]) regardless of skill, and backups (RB2, FB, WR4, TE2) play every snap with no rotation, fatigue or in-game injuries (injuries are rolled only after the game). | Pick the best Yac/Speed returner and rotate backups. |  |
| QA-605 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSimPlays.cs:34 | TimeOfPossession counts only clock burned inside plays of the same quarter (`_gs.Quarter == qBefore`), so verified totals are about 58.6 of 60 minutes and the team on the field at a quarter break loses seconds. | Credit runoff and the end-of-period seconds to the possessing team. |  |
| QA-606 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/QuickSim.cs:77 | SimPlayer.Compress flattens every rating by 0.4 around 68 (SimTuning.Sens), so game variance is low: verified 272 games with 0 shutouts and 0 games of 50+ points, total-points p10/p50/p90 of 29/42/58, top WR 1,145 yards and top QB 29 TDs. | Raise Sens or add per-game variance for blowouts and shutouts. |  |
| QA-607 | open | S3 | SIM | Rules | Core/RuleSet.cs:49 | Pro regular-season OT is a single 10-minute period with ties allowed, producing about 0.7-1.5 percent tied games (4 in one verified 272-game season) versus about 0.3 percent in the NFL. | Add the rule detail that shortens OT ties (or allow a second sudden-death phase). |  |
| QA-610 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | League/Offseason.cs:127 | Free agency junk filter retires every FA below 46 overall (or age 34+ below 66) and the pool is cut to 300 each year (line 200), so the market is thin so signed veterans are thin; retired list also stores players who never played. | Keep a larger FA pool and only retire players who actually left the game. |  |
| QA-616 | open | S3 | UI | Engine/AI | Core/PlayCallModel.cs:191 | FiltersFor offers the same ZONE/MAN/BLITZ (defense) or RUN/PASS/PLAY ACTION/SCREEN (offense) filters for every formation, so verified filtering Goal Line Defense by BLITZ (or Goal Line offense by SCREEN) shows an empty play page. | Offer only filters that have at least one play in the current set. |  |
| QA-623 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:582,585 | Once the QB has crossed the LOS HandleReads is disabled forever and there is no illegal-forward-pass penalty, so the CPU QB never throws after crossing the line but a user QB can via TryUserThrow only when !QbHasCrossedLos; an AI QB at LosY+0.3 while the ball is thrown 2 s later is impossible but a QB who steps back behind the LOS can't regain throwing rights (flag never reset). | Reset QbHasCrossedLos when he returns behind the LOS and add IllegalForwardPass. |  |
| QA-624 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | OffenseAI.Qb.cs:719-745 | UserQbInput: pressing a throw in Dropback only works after S.Time>0.5 and the button is ignored during Mesh/Fake; a user QB on a play action cannot throw until the fake ends and there is no feedback that the input was dropped. | Queue the throw request until the state allows it. |  |
| QA-625 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | CarrierMoves.cs:264 | ConsiderAIMoves rolls Rng.NextFloat every tick with a 4.5% pass-through, consuming RNG draws proportional to time near defenders; any change in tick timing (e.g. frame hitch with fixed steps) is deterministic but tuning moves means retuning every downstream RNG sequence. | Use a per-carrier timer for decision cadence. |  |
| QA-627 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | PhysicalGameSim.cs:265-271 | WantsTwoPoint ignores the 'go for 2 at 9, 10, 13, 14 etc.' chart and uses a flat 2% rate otherwise; with diff already updated after the TD, -1/-2/-5 are the only cases checked. | Use a standard two-point decision table. |  |
| QA-629 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | BlockEngagement.cs:237-238 | BlockerScore gives WR/CB/FS/SS a floor of 35 and non-OL on defense/ST a floor of 45, but not TE/RB/FB/LB, so a TE with RunBlocking 20 loses to every DL and a CB gets a higher floor than an FB. | Add TE/FB/RB to the floor logic or use ratings consistently. |  |
| QA-630 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:546 | OnPassThrown predicts the catch spot at z=1.6 only; for a ball that lands short or is tipped the 'T' is the landing point, and Responsibility is computed against that point, so deep safeties are not triggered by overthrown balls that stay in the air past their zone. | Compute T from the target's expected catch point as well. |  |
| QA-632 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:399 | PunterUpdate walks the punter forward at 3 yd/s for the 0.5 s hold (2 steps) but the punt launch point is `k.Pos + (0,0.8)` so the ball moves 0.8 yd ahead of him; if the punter takes damage/steps the kick point is behind the LOS by less than the formation depth, and the facing is fixed (0,1) so he never turns toward a rusher or the sideline for a directional punt. | Aim the punter toward the AimX target and release from the foot position. |  |
| QA-633 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:555-558 | FG block chance uses the nearest defender within 2.4 yards of the HOLDER (not of the kicking line), and a successful block marks Fumbled=true on top of BlockKick's own increment (S.FumbleCount/Fumbled are bumped twice: BlockKick increments, then `S.Fumbled = true` again), so FumbleCount is 1 here but UpdateFieldGoalFlight's swat path increments it separately; fumble stats on blocked kicks are inconsistent. | Count the block once and use a dedicated KickBlocked flag, not Fumbled. |  |
| QA-634 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:561-575 | Field-goal kick speed vmax = 23.5 + 10.5*kp with the launch angle theta = clamp(0.78 - dist0*0.0045, 0.50, 0.72) and the flight always passes the post at z=5 yd (15 ft) if the leg is strong enough; distance control is binary (reaches or falls short), kicks that are good always clear the crossbar by 5 ft and the lateral error is only an angle, so 'wide left/right' vs 'short' vs 'doink' shape is limited; there is also no wind or rush-timing effect beyond the sd. | Add trajectory scatter in z (clear by 0-3 yd) and wind. |  |
| QA-635 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:564 | FG accuracy sd = lerp(0.094, 0.036, acc) * (1 + 0.012*(dist-40)) radians; at 50 yd the sd is only ~14% larger than at 40, but the angular half-width of the posts shrinks from 4.4 deg to 3.5 deg, so 50+ yard make rate with a 70 accuracy kicker is about 75% (NFL ~ 65% at 50-59 and ~50% 55+); long kicks are too reliable. | Increase distance penalty and add a power requirement margin (vReq close to vmax raises sd). |  |
| QA-637 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:259 | The kickoff target is clamped to [1, Width-1] laterally but the Y target LosY+dist is not clamped to the back of the end zone, so a 74-yard kick from the 35 targets Y=109+ (past the back line is 110) and balls with latErr can land out the back; the touchback path handles Y>109 via `ball.Pos.Y > 109f`, giving a too-generous touchback for a ball that lands OOB. | Clamp dist so the target is at most 109 and handle OOB-through-the-end-zone explicitly. |  |
| QA-638 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:252-255 | Kickoff distance is 52 + 22*kp (+-2.5) yards from the kicking spot, so with a 35-yard kickoff line every kick of kp>=0.6 lands in the end zone (87+13): touchback rate is dominated by kicking power; there is no directional kick/pooch variety besides explicit Squib/Rugby plays. | Add a kickoff aimed-depth parameter to the AI call and reduce the baseline distance. |  |
| QA-639 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | SpecialTeamsAI.cs:879-889 | BuildFieldGoalResult: PlayResult.FieldGoal(S.KickGood...) is computed from S.KickDistance which is set in DoFieldGoal before a block; for a blocked kick KickDistance remains the nominal distance and the 'FieldGoalMissed' result is built with a Turnover/BallSide state that can yield a returned-kick yardage when the defense recovers a block (r.ReturnYards) but the spot rule (ball dead where recovered) is not applied. | Spot a blocked FG per rule (recovery spot or LOS) and add KickBlocked to the result. |  |
| QA-641 | open | S2 | SIM | Engine/AI | DefenseAI.cs:213 + TackleResolver.cs:196-253 | After a fumble the carrier is Down but the offense/defense AI branches on `S.Carrier` (null for loose ball) and PursueBall gets every defender, including pass rushers and DB deep in coverage, running straight at the ball at vmax, so a fumble on the line is recovered by the closest of 22 players within a few frames and a fumble near the sideline is recovered before it can roll out. | Only the 4-5 nearest react immediately; others pursue the carrier/ball with the normal read delay. |  |
| QA-642 | open | S3 | SIM | Engine/AI | CatchResolver.cs:47-54 | ReachRadius uses Facing (which lags the intended turn by TurnRate) to shrink the catch radius to radius+0.30 when the ball is 'behind the shoulders'; at 12 rad/s turn limits a sprinting receiver looking back for the ball is often still facing downfield for ~10 frames, so back-shoulder throws and comebacks are dropped far more often than the numeric catch probability suggests. | Use the head/torso turn rate or ignore facing for the first 0.2 s after the pass. |  |

| QA-076 | open | S2 | GFX | Graphics/Lighting | look_day_clear_3_player_portrait_v05.png, look_day_clear_4_helmet_closeup_v05.png | Daytime v0.5 look: harsh direct sun leaves 60 percent of the players pitch black in shadow with crushed detail (helmets, faces, jersey backs), no fill light, while highlights on the white jerseys blow to grey-white; the contrast is unreadable (the opposite of the clean, bright 2K8 reference). | Add a sky/fill ambient and lower the sun contrast during gameplay lighting. |  |

| QA-095 | open | S2 | GFX | Graphics/Lighting | v3_college_hash.png | The college rule set draws a THIRD line of dashed hash marks/yard ticks (three parallel dash rows at y=440, 480, 600, 660) while the pro rule set shows two: dashed rows are misaligned with the row of yard numerals, the lower row is a different dash size/colour (grey), the LOS blue line is not drawn on this frame, so college markings look like leftover artefacts. | Rebuild the college hash/tick markings and draw the LOS in both rule sets. |  |

| QA-255 | open | S2 | GFX | Graphics/Lighting | run3/match_carry_2.png | The entire frame (even the stationary scoreboard-lines and field numerals) is smeared by a heavy blur: motion blur/DoF is applied to the full pitch while the camera is on the ground carrier; players are unrecognisable 25-px blobs and the LOS/first-down lines are fuzzy. | Disable camera motion blur in gameplay and cap DoF at the broadcast distance. |  |

The weave gradient is wrong (noisy/tilted, shimmering jerseys). PixImportPresets only applies to NEW assets. | Set NormalMap type with sRGB off, reimport, and add a test that classifies every *_Normal texture's meta. |  |

Line 68 (props cap 2048) runs after the cap was applied at line 40, so it has no effect. | Validate type/sRGB for all textures, move the Props cap before line 40. |  |
