Line 206 has an identical ternary on both branches. | Use explicit lists or folder conventions and remove the dead ternary. |  |
| QA-963 | open | S3 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Tools/V6ArtBuilder.cs:53 and 125 | All generated data textures (number atlas with fill/outline in R/G, letter atlas, scuff, flood cookie) are saved with TextureImporterCompression.Compressed and mipmaps on; BC1 block compression on 1-pixel outlines and per-cell mip bleed between atlas cells blurs jersey digits at distance. | Use uncompressed or BC7 for the atlases, disable mips beyond cell size or add padding. |  |
| QA-964 | open | S3 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Tools/V6ArtBuilder.cs:257 | Dead expression `(Rnd() < 0.5f ? 0f : 3.1416f * 0f)` adds nothing, so all 52000 turf blades lean the same way (+-0.55 rad around one direction) and the micro normal shows directional streaks across the pitch. | Randomize lean direction and re-bake PIX_TurfMicro. |  |
| QA-965 | open | S2 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Tools/V6ArtBuilder.cs:225-230 and 44-55 | CopyTex loads each licensed texture (some 80 MB PNGs) into a CPU canvas, re-encodes it and re-imports with wrap Repeat, mips and aniso 8; helmet maps (UV islands) with Repeat wrap and Compressed format bleed across island edges at distance, producing seams on the shell. | Use Clamp for UV-island textures and dilate borders before compression. |  |
| QA-966 | open | S2 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Tools/V6ArtBuilder.cs:566-600 (BuildHelmetMaterial) | The clear-coat keyword is enabled by EnableKeyword(_MATERIAL_FEATURE_CLEAR_COAT) without setting the material-type properties HDRP derives it from, so the next ResetMaterialKeywords or inspector refresh drops the clear coat and the helmet loses its gloss; _NormalScale 0.35 undercuts the baked helmet detail. | Set the HDRP material type float and run HDShaderUtils.ResetMaterialKeywords last. |  |
| QA-967 | open | S3 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Assets/MaterialFactory.cs:102-130 | FixPinkMaterials resets every unsupported material to HDRP/Lit with _BaseColor white and smoothness 0.25 and discards other properties (emission, alpha, masks); crowd/skin/hair materials can lose their look and the repair is silent apart from the count. | Log each repaired material and keep alpha/mask maps. |  |
| QA-968 | open | S2 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Assets/PlayerPrefabBuilder.cs:107-108 | PIX_FaceMask is a solid dark grey (0.09, 0.09, 0.1) metallic 0.9 with no texture and the visor a flat 45 percent alpha dark blue; facemasks render as black blobs without the licensed face-mask texture, and the transparent visor is created without depth-write/sorting settings for HDRP transparent. | Use the licensed face-mask materials/textures and set HDRP transparent sort priority. |  |
| QA-969 | open | S3 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Licensed/DraftFever/Props/Field/grassMarkings_NEW.png.meta and outsideGrassMarkings.png.meta | Original field marking textures use aniso 1 and 2048 max size (source 4096), so thin yard lines blur at the broadcast camera's grazing angle; the V6 copy uses aniso 8 but the legacy materials (Legacy look switch) still use these. | Raise aniso to 8-16 and max size 4096 on markings. |  |
| QA-971 | open | S1 | GFX | Graphics/Lighting | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Build/SceneBuilder.cs:107-126 and Assets/_Project/Scenes/Match_Pro.unity (Flood_* lights) | Floodlights are 12 spot lights at 9,400,000 cd (side) and 7,200,000 cd (ends) at 44-46 m height; summing inverse-square illuminance at midfield gives roughly 10,000 lux, not the ~1500 lux the code comment (and WeatherPresets' 1800 lux estimate) assume. WeatherSystem.cs:97-151 uses these scene values as the base, so night turf sits ~3 stops over the fixed exposure (EV 8.55 meters about 900 lux): washed-out, flat, un-cinematic night. | Re-derive candela from the target 1500-2000 lux (about 1.5-2M cd per lamp), keep falloff, and lower exposure/contrast so highlights roll off instead of clipping. |  |
| QA-972 | open | S1 | GFX | Graphics/Lighting | Assets/_Project/Settings/Volumes/Night_Stadium.asset (IndirectLightingController 5, GradientSky multiplier 120, fixedExposure 8.55) and SceneBuilder.cs:86-93 | Night profile multiplies indirect diffuse by 5, uses a bright gradient sky (multiplier 120) and a fixed 8.55 exposure, so ambient fill is high and shadows are lifted; combined with no shadows on any floodlight the night game looks evenly lit and flat rather than dramatic. | Lower indirect multiplier to ~1-2, darken sky, give the key lamps shadows and raise contrast/exposure range for pools of light. |  |
| QA-973 | open | S2 | GFX | Graphics/Lighting | Assets/_Project/Scenes/Match_Pro.unity and Stadium_Pro.unity (all 12 Flood lights, shadowType 0) | None of the 12 floodlights cast shadows in the saved scene (WeatherSystem enables rim lamps only by graphics tier), so players have a single soft moon shadow at night and no multi-directional shadows under the lights, the biggest missing cue for stadium drama. | Enable shadows on 2-4 key lamps for High/Ultra and use a shadow atlas budget in PixHDRP.asset. |  |
| QA-974 | open | S2 | GFX | Graphics/Lighting | Assets/_Project/Settings/Volumes/Day_Stadium.asset, Night_Stadium.asset, Menu_Stage.asset | ColorAdjustments (contrast 10, saturation 8, colorFilter 1/0.965/0.915), WhiteBalance 9, Vignette 0.2, FilmGrain 0.1, MotionBlur 0.12, SSAO 0.7 are identical in day, night and menu: the saved profiles carry one warm grade for every time of day (night gets the same warm filter), and only the runtime volume in WeatherSystem differentiates. In the editor and in any scene without WeatherSystem the look is flat. | Author distinct grades per profile (cool shadows at night, stronger vignette/contrast) and sync them with WeatherPresets. |  |
| QA-975 | open | S2 | GFX | Graphics/Lighting | Assets/_Project/Settings/Volumes/Day_Stadium.asset, Night_Stadium.asset, Menu_Stage.asset (HDShadowSettings) | cascadeShadowBorder0..3 are 0 and the splits are 0.05 / 0.15 of a 160 m max distance with 3 cascades, so the last cascade spans 24 to 160 m on a 2048 px sun map (maxDirectionalShadowMapResolution in PixHDRP.asset): most players in the broadcast view are shadowed by a ~6 cm/texel cascade, giving blurry, crawling contact shadows and visible cascade seams. | Cap max shadow distance at ~80 m, add cascade borders (0.1-0.2), use 4 cascades on High/Ultra and a 4096 sun map. |  |
| QA-976 | open | S2 | GFX | Graphics/Lighting | Assets/_Project/Settings/PixHDRP.asset (supportDitheringCrossFade 0) vs ProjectSettings/QualitySettings.asset (enableLODCrossFade 1) | HDRP LOD cross-fade is disabled in the pipeline asset while the quality levels request it; stadium LODGroups pop instead of fading, and the player gear/body LOD swap (PlayerLod) cannot use dithered fade. | Enable supportDitheringCrossFade or remove the misleading quality flags. |  |
| QA-990 | open | S3 | GFX | Graphics/Lighting | Assets/_Project/Settings/Volumes/Menu_Stage.asset (Fog meanFreePath 40, albedo 0.3/0.4/0.6, fixedExposure 8.4) | The menu profile uses a very thick volumetric fog (mean free path 40 m) with fixed exposure 8.4 over a plane floor; the menu hero and UI sit in a bright blue haze and the volumetric budget is paid for a static scene. | Reduce density or bake the haze as a sprite for the menu. |  |
| QA-1035 | open | S2 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Assets/ExtraPrefabBuilder.cs:17-24 | Assign overwrites every material slot of every renderer with a single material (referee, crowd, accessories, coaches share the pattern), so multi-material meshes (hair/skin/uniform sub-meshes) lose their separate textures. BuildHelmets additionally applies New_Helmet_MAT to the Spartan and Vintage helmet FBX in Helmets/New/Medium although their UVs are different from the Updated helmet maps. | Map materials per slot name and pass a per-model texture set for the Spartan/Vintage helmets. |  |
| QA-1036 | open | S3 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Assets/ExtraPrefabBuilder.cs:59-85 | Recolor identifies the crowd mask colours by Manhattan distance (40-55) in sRGB on a texture that is later compressed, so anti-aliased and compressed edge pixels miss the match and keep the salmon / magenta key colours (coloured halos), and the replacement brightness uses only the mask colour's luminance, so recoloured clothing is flat. Team colours are hard-coded (crimson and white) instead of the team data. | Match in HSV with a tolerance, keep the albedo shading, and read team colours from teams.csv. |  |
| QA-1038 | open | S3 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Build/CrowdBuilder.cs:35-44 | The crowd palettes are fixed navy/gold, crimson, white, black, blue, grey, gold and mixed, not the home or away team colours, and the atlas is baked once; every stadium crowd looks the same whatever the matchup, and the "home navy/gold" first palette clashes with most pro teams. | Tint the crowd atlas at runtime from the two team colours (MaterialPropertyBlock) or bake per-team palettes. |  |
| QA-1044 | open | S2 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Tools/V6ArtBuilder.cs:513-516 (placements) and Docs/QA/graphics_notes.md (Known issues) | Jersey number placements are hand-converted pixel coordinates (e.g. numberShoulderL rotation Pi, numberShoulderR 0) and the right sleeve cap numeral is documented as reading upside down at some poses; the placement vectors are shared by all three body sizes although the Small torso has a different topology (3124 vs 3067 vertices, one extra collar island). | Re-derive placements per size from bake_player_regions.py --compare output and add a render test. |  |
| QA-1046 | open | S3 | GFX | Materials | Assets/_Project/Shaders/PixLit/PixLit.shader:270 vs Assets/_Project/Scripts/Art/PixLookAssets.cs:33 | The shader default _PixHelmetDecal is (0.5, 0.05, 0.05, 1) but PixLookAssets.helmetDecal defaults to (0.5, 0.06, 0.05, 1): any helmet material that is not driven by PlayerAvatar.SetupHelmetFrame (editor renders, replay avatars, QA shots) shows the crest 1 percent of the shell height lower than in the match. | Keep one source of the default (the ScriptableObject) and set it on material creation. |  |

## 7. Stats and Box Score (20)

| ID | Status | Sev | Track | Category | Where | Issue | Fix | Verified by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| QA-057 | open | S2 | SIM | Stats | Logs/qa_audit/run3/boxscore.txt vs match_final.png | Box score says AWAY (MIA) pass yards 143 while the Player of the Game screen shows the MIA QB with 159 passing yards; sacks (4) are not subtracted consistently, or yards are credited twice. | Make the team and player totals come from the same source. |  |
| QA-107 | open | S2 | UI | Stats | Docs/Screens/v030_college_final.png | College OT final screen: line score shows MIA 0-0-0-0-3 (13) and BUF 0-0-0-0-6 (16) while TEAM STATS shows total yards -6 vs 9, passing -8 vs 12, rushing 2 vs -3, 0 first downs and 0 turnovers for both: a 16-13 game with 9 and -6 total yards and no first downs,; a team that scored 13 points (OT field goal plus earlier scoring) with -6 total yards and no first downs is implausible. | Check yard accounting and scoring-drive starting field position (turnovers 0 so scores must come from long returns or penalties). |  |
| QA-136 | open | S2 | UI | Stats | bc_halftime_performers.png | G. PAGANO is credited "4 TKL 4 SCK" in the first half (every tackle a sack) while Miami's best defender has "1 TKL 0 SCK"; unrealistic (real elite DE ~1 sack a game). | Re-tune pass rush win rate and credit sacks to rushers only. |  |
| QA-169 | open | S2 | UI | Stats | v020_match_final.png | Team stats show Miami 187 total yards (177 rushing) with only 1 first down, and Buffalo 12 yards with 1 first down; 187 yards cannot produce 1 first down, so first-down counting is wrong (the tracker misses first downs from yardage/penalty). | Count a first down whenever the line to gain is reached (rush, pass, penalty automatic). |  |
| QA-170 | open | S2 | UI | Stats | v030_college_final.png | Quarter line score shows Miami 0-0-0-0-3 but a final of 13, Buffalo 0-0-0-0-6 but 16: the line score and the final score come from different sources and do not add up. | Derive the final score from the line score (or vice versa) and assert equality. |  |
| QA-171 | open | S3 | UI | Stats | v030_college_final.png | Team stats table has no sacks, completions, time of possession, 3rd-down or red-zone rows; both teams show 0 first downs and 0 turnovers in an overtime game. | Add the standard rows and verify the numbers. |  |
| QA-184 | open | S2 | SIM | Stats | Logs/autotest_bc6/autotest.txt (tracker lines) | Drive log reads "3 PLAYS - -13 YARDS - 0:48 -> Punt" and "4 PLAYS - -6 YARDS -> Downs": drives with negative yards twice in one 52-play game, drive 3 "8 PLAYS - 93 YARDS" for a 117-yard team total; yards per drive are wildly bimodal. | Check yard accounting for sacks/losses and big plays. |  |
| QA-280 | open | S2 | SIM | Stats | run3/Player.log | In a 4-quarter test game the clock shows each possession change at an inconsistent time: BUF's score of the Q1 first down is logged "FIRST DOWN Q1 0:32 BUF ball 1&10 @67" and the next presnap says "MIA 33" (ball spotted from the other team's perspective): the field position format flips between "@67" and "MIA 33", so the internal spot is not the same as the displayed spot. | Use a single yard-line convention in logs and displays. |  |
| QA-305 | open | S2 | SIM | Stats | Logs/autotest*/boxscore.txt | The box score header reports "tackle events 21 / sacks 4" for 24 snaps and "first downs 1" for one team yet its yards line says AWAY yards -11: the box score has no completions/attempts, no rushing attempts, no time of possession and no per-quarter score for a PAT/two-point. | Extend the box score to the standard categories and verify totals. |  |
| QA-761 | open | S2 | UI | Stats | Gameplay/Match/MatchFlow.cs:399 | The final-screen TeamStats (separate from GameTracker) ignore Interception and Fumble plays for yards and Plays, and credit penalty yards from Def.ProYards even under college rules (Core/Penalty.cs:96 picks CollegeYards), so TOTAL YARDS and PENALTIES on the final screen disagree with the TV box score and the real enforcement. | Count every play type, use the enforced penalty yardage from the outcome and drive the final screen from one stat source (GameTracker). |  |
| QA-834 | open | S2 | UI | Stats | Presentation/Stats/GameTracker.cs:256 | Drives are ended on every PeriodChanged, including Q1->Q2 and Q3->Q4 where the same team keeps the ball, so one drive becomes two records ("END OF QUARTER" result), Drives / box.Drives are double counted, and WorthSummary can pop a drive-summary card mid-drive with result "END OF QUARTER". | End drives only at the half / game end (Quarter 2 or the last period) or on a real change of possession, and carry the open drive across quarter breaks. |  |
| QA-835 | open | S2 | UI | Stats | Presentation/Stats/GameTracker.cs:173 | PlayType.Fumble adds no RushAtt / RushYds / PassYds to the player or team box (only Current.Yards += r.Yards), so fumbled carries lose their attempts and yards in TOTAL YARDS and RUSHING YARDS while the drive summary still counts them; the fumbler is looked up by RusherId only so QB and receiver fumbles are credited to nobody. | Credit the carry/target yardage before recording the fumble and look up the fumbler from the right id (Rusher/Receiver/Passer). |  |
| QA-836 | open | S3 | UI | Stats | Presentation/Stats/GameTracker.cs:305 | StatLine.ForcedFumbles / FumbleRecoveries are weighted in Metric() and LeaderLine but never incremented anywhere in RecordPlay, and tackles are credited as solo only (no assists, no TFL on sacks), so the DEFENSE leaders and Player of the Game ignore forced fumbles and recoveries. | Credit the stripper and the recoverer from the play events. |  |
| QA-837 | open | S3 | UI | Stats | Presentation/Stats/GameTracker.cs:193 | PAT / two-point points are added to Drives[Drives.Count-1], i.e. the last started drive, so after a pick-six or kick-return touchdown the extra point is credited to the drive of the team that just gave the ball away (and defensive TDs add 6 points to no drive at all). | Attribute points to the scoring team's drive (or to a separate non-offense scoring list). |  |
| QA-838 | open | S3 | UI | Stats | Presentation/Stats/GameTracker.cs:229 | ScoringEvent stores Quarter = QuarterBefore but ClockSeconds = after.ClockSeconds; a touchdown on the last play of a quarter is logged with the next quarter's reset clock (e.g. Q1 15:00). | Store the clock at the moment of the score (or the pre-play clock). |  |
| QA-840 | open | S2 | UI | Stats | Presentation/Stats/GameTracker.cs:98 | Penalty yards are Math.Abs(Def.ProYards) regardless of RuleSet (College rules use CollegeYards, Core/Penalty.cs:96), spot fouls (DPI) and half-the-distance enforcement, so PENALTIES and the lower-third show the nominal yards, not the enforced ones (BroadcastDirector.cs:334 and MatchFlow.cs:399 do the same). | Use the enforced yardage from the PlayOutcome / PenaltyEnforcer. |  |
| QA-841 | open | S3 | UI | Stats | Presentation/Stats/GameTracker.cs:109 | `_have0` is never reset after RecordPlay, so if BeginPlay is skipped for a play (no snapshot) the drive StartSpot / StartClock come from the previous play, and OT periods use regulation QuarterSeconds for Elapsed (L61) so time of possession in overtime is wrong. | Reset _have0 after each play and use the real period length. |  |
| QA-842 | open | S3 | UI | Stats | Presentation/Stats/GameTracker.cs:55 | TeamIdxOfPlayer returns 0 whenever Home.Id == Away.Id (mirror match / CPU test), so every away player is booked to the home team and Leaders() merge both teams. | Key lines by team index instead of team id. |  |
| QA-843 | open | S3 | UI | Stats | Presentation/Stats/OverlayText.cs:82 | StatLine for a kicker with only extra points returns "" (the FG branch requires FgAtt > 0) so PAT-only kickers get no lower-third line; the helper named Pct (L123) returns "a/b" not a percentage and there is no 4TH DOWN row in Compare(). | Add an XP branch, rename Pct, add 4th down. |  |
| QA-890 | open | S3 | UI | Stats | UI/MatchHud.cs:504 | The final-screen TEAM STATS table lists 6 rows (yards, first downs, turnovers, penalties) but omits sacks, completions/attempts, third-down conversions and time of possession, all of which the TV compare card (OverlayText.cs:126) and TeamBoxScore track, and the rows use the other (MatchFlow) stat source. | Build the final rows from OverlayText.Compare so both views agree. |  |

| QA-033 | open | S3 | UI | Text | match_presnap_1.png | Far-sideline yard numerals are inverted 180 degrees ("OI", "0S") so they read upside down from the main camera and their "10/20" arrows point to the wrong side; for a sideline camera it is conventional that far-side numbers read upside down, but here near-side and far-side numerals differ in size (near ~3x bigger) giving a distorted look. | Equalise size/spacing of both numeral rows in world space and verify arrow direction toward the nearest goal line. |  |

| QA-462 | open | S3 | SIM | Determinism | BlockerAI.cs:173,DefenseAI.cs:97,BlockerAI.cs:46,57-58 | List<T>.Sort (introsort, unstable) is used on lists with ties (pair costs, StartPos.X equals when slots are mirrored/pinched), so the resulting assignment order may differ between the Mono, IL2CPP and Editor runtimes and between .NET versions; replay/determinism digests (PlayEventLog.Digest) can diverge across platforms. | Use a stable sort (OrderBy or an index tie-break) for assignment lists. |  |

| QA-857 | open | S3 | GFX | UI/Flow | Presentation/World/FirstPersonFx.cs:32 | The facemask texture is generated per pixel at 1280x720 during Attach (about 0.9M iterations of smoothstep/sqrt, a load hitch) and then stretched full-screen with no aspect correction, so on 21:9 and 4:3 the bars and posts are distorted and on 4K the 8 px bars blur. | Generate it at screen size / use a 9-sliced mask sprite and anchor bars proportionally. |  |

| QA-863 | open | S3 | UI | UI/Flow | Presentation/Replay/ReplayController.cs:163 | PlayReel passes skippable=false to RunSegment and polls `skip()` only after each 3-4 s clip finishes, so A/B/Menu during a highlight clip does nothing until the clip ends. | Poll skip inside the segment loop. |  |
| QA-866 | open | S3 | CAM | UI/Flow | Presentation/Replay/ReplayView.cs:61 | The Sideline replay angle puts the camera at x = down*28.5 (side flips with the play direction) while live shots use ShotContext.LiveSide, so replays of plays moving toward -Z are shot from the opposite sideline than the live TV camera and look mirrored. | Use the live broadcast side (LiveSide) for the sideline angle. |  |
| QA-868 | open | S3 | GFX | UI/Flow | Presentation/Director/BroadcastCamera.cs:83 | Every cut switches antialiasing from TAA to FXAA for 3 frames, producing a visible aliasing pop/flicker on each broadcast cut and replay angle change. | Set HDAdditionalCameraData.resetPostProcessingHistory for the cut frame instead of swapping the antialiasing mode. |  |

| QA-885 | open | S2 | UI | Text | UI/MatchHud.cs:168 | The banner headline is 92 px NoWrap Overflow in a 1160 px banner; timeout banners are built as TeamName(...) + " TIMEOUT" (MatchDirector.cs:511,550) so "San Francisco 49ers TIMEOUT" or "Washington Commanders TIMEOUT" (about 1300-1500 px, and mixed case because TeamName is not upper-cased) spill past both banner edges. | Auto-size the headline (min about 56 px) or use the 3-letter abbreviation / nickname for the headline. |  |

| QA-887 | open | S2 | UI | UI/Flow | UI/MatchHud.cs:521 | Final screen is laid out for a 1080 px tall canvas: the CONTINUE button is at bottom 70..170 and the stat rows run to y=-866; on 21:9 (canvas height about 935) the button's top (765 from top) overlaps the last two stat rows. | Anchor the stats block between the rule and the button using a vertical layout. |  |

New animations will work in the editor but be absent from shipped builds. | Derive the whitelist from keys referenced in AnimKit/StanceRules/ActionAnimator (test), log every dropped key, and raise the cap for stance loops. |  |

| QA-1025 | open | S3 | UI | Tools | Tools/unity.ps1:1-3 and 10-13 | Usage text says `[-Quit]` but the switch is -NoQuit; the script assigns to the automatic variable $args, never creates the Logs folder (Unity then writes no log), prints "exit N" but does not call exit with the code so callers cannot detect failures, and matches the editor by the folder name text PIX PRO FOOTBALL in the command line only. | Fix the usage, ensure Logs exists, `exit $p.ExitCode`, use a different variable name. |  |
| QA-1026 | open | S3 | UI | Tools | Tools/unity_wait_run.ps1 and Tools/Tripo/*.ps1, Tools/Blender/make_sheets.ps1, run_lookdev.sh, render_all_new.sh | Absolute paths to C:\Users\peyto\... and Unity/Blender install locations are hard-coded in eight scripts, so a second machine, CI or any contributor cannot run them; unity_wait_run.ps1 does not forward the exit code. | Use $PSScriptRoot-relative roots and a UNITY_PATH/BLENDER_PATH variable. |  |
| QA-1027 | open | S3 | UI | Tools | Tools/Blender/build_all.ps1:12-25 | No $ErrorActionPreference=Stop or $LASTEXITCODE check after any of the seven Blender runs (build_v6_player_art.ps1 does check), the Referee2.fbx source is copied from a _AssetSource backup folder name ("FBXs backup"), and paint_out_logos boxes are hard-coded pixel rectangles for one texture. | Stop on error and log missing sources. |  |
| QA-1028 | open | S3 | MDL | Tools | Tools/Blender/build_v6_player_art.ps1:30-34 | The V6 helper script still builds LODs with pix_player_lods.py (FBX output under Logs) and never runs pix_player_fix.py, model_qa_measure.py or model_qa_weights.py, so the documented one-command rebuild does not produce the .pixmesh bodies BodyMeshImporter needs. | Add the fix and QA steps with a measurement gate. |  |
| QA-1029 | open | S3 | MDL | Tools | Tools/Blender/pix_player_common.py:147-168 and pix_player_fix.py:256 | Requires Blender 5.x APIs (me.corner_normals, image_settings.media_type, a Blender 5.2 install path) with no version check; load_png returns a (array, channels) tuple although its docstring says it returns the array, and save_png with bit_depth 16 changes the scene's view transform to Raw as a side effect. | Assert bpy.app.version at start and fix the docstring. |  |
| QA-1033 | open | S2 | ANI | Build | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/BuildScript.cs:36-47 | SwapInSlimLibrary copies the current Resources/ArtLibrary.asset over Library/PIXBackup before swapping; if a previous build crashed (the finally never ran) the Resources file is already the slim library, so the next run overwrites the only backup of the FULL library with the slim one and the full registry is lost until ArtLibraryBuilder is run again. | Refuse to swap when a backup exists, or build the slim library at a separate path and use a build preprocessor instead of overwriting. |  |
| QA-1034 | open | S3 | ANI | Build | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/BuildScript.cs:56 | The slim filter drops any clip over 12 s (spin_move 25 s, keys containing "stance" 50 s) and caps 6 variants per key in registration order, not by quality; keys like tackle_success from the 20 s+ BigHits retarget packs or any BoNES take over 12 s vanish from builds and the first 6 variants are whatever sorted first by name. | Cut clips to their playable window in ClipCleaner first and pick variants explicitly. |  |
| QA-1049 | open | S3 | UI | Tools | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Tools/StanceGallery.cs:81 and 443, ClipFacingAudit.cs:22, ModelQaRenderer.cs:27, StickSheet.cs:52 | QA tools write generated PNG sets into the documentation tree (Docs/QA/stance_gallery 711 MB, Docs/QA/models, Docs/QA/camera, Docs/Screens/anim) instead of Logs/, mixing 0.9 GB of renders and the audit CSV into folders that are meant to be committed. | Default the output to Logs/ and copy selected evidence into Docs by hand. |  |

| ID | Status | Sev | Track | Category | Where | Issue | Fix | Verified by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| QA-122 | open | S2 | UI | Input | bc_manual_behind_qb.png | Replay legend shows four identical D-pad glyphs for REWIND, FORWARD, DEPTH OF FIELD, GROUND LEVEL with no direction; impossible to tell which press does what. | Use directional D-pad glyphs (left, right, up, down). |  |
| QA-162 | open | S2 | UI | Input | v030_presnap_default.png | At the line the hint bar shows two identical D-pad glyphs for SLIDE and NEW PLAY; the LB pill touches the word FLIP. | Use directional D-pad glyphs and add 8 px padding after pills. |  |
| QA-301 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | ProjectSettings/ProjectSettings.asset:90 (runInBackground: 0) | The game stops updating when its window loses focus (alt-tab, notification, second monitor click): in the first autotest run the player stalled at the coin toss for 120 s because the window never had focus ("FAIL: match stalled for 120 s at '' phase CoinToss"), and automated/online tools can never run it unattended. | Enable runInBackground (or auto-pause with a pause menu on focus loss) so match state, audio and UI stay consistent. |  |
| QA-744 | open | S2 | UI | Settings | UI/MatchHud.cs:568 | The pause menu Lighting selector ignores the chosen index and just fires LightingToggleRequested, so picking "Night game" while it is already night toggles to Day, and the selector label can disagree with the real lighting (WeatherSystem also owns the roots). | Pass the chosen LightingPreset to the director (Lighting.Apply((LightingPreset)i)) and do nothing when it equals Lighting.Current. |  |
| QA-745 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | UI/MatchHud.cs:567 | Pause Camera selector (and every Tab/View press at MatchDirector.cs:346,460,569) overwrites PixSettings.DefaultCamera without Save, so a temporary in-match camera silently changes the Settings > Default camera preference for the next game. | Keep the live camera mode separate from the saved default; only write DefaultCamera from the Settings screen. |  |
| QA-751 | open | S2 | UI | Input | UI/MatchHud.cs:573 | The in-match pause footer always draws Xbox glyph_a / glyph_menu / glyph_analog_all even when the keyboard is the active device (MatchInput.GamepadActive is ignored here), so keyboard players see controller buttons. | Build the footer through UiPrompts.ButtonIcon (scheme aware) and rebuild it when the scheme flips. |  |
| QA-752 | open | S2 | UI | Input | UI/PauseMenu.cs:51 | The free-roam pause footer shows only Xbox glyphs (A SELECT, B RESUME) regardless of the active device. | Use the scheme-aware prompt helper for every footer. |  |
| QA-753 | open | S2 | UI | Input | UI/MainMenuController.cs:91 | Main menu footer always shows glyph_a SELECT / glyph_b BACK on keyboard and mouse too, and BACK is shown on the top-level menu where B does nothing. | Use scheme-aware prompts and hide BACK on the root menu. |  |
| QA-755 | open | S2 | UI | Input | UI/MatchHud.cs:564 | Pressing A on RESUME is a UI Submit processed by the EventSystem before the director coroutine runs in the same frame; the director then reads MatchInput.Down[A] on the resumed frame (MatchDirector.cs:351 confirms the highlighted play, the pre-snap loop snaps on A), so resuming can also confirm the play or snap the ball. | Swallow gameplay input for one frame after unpausing. |  |
| QA-771 | open | S3 | UI | Input | Editor/Build/RuntimeAssetsBuilder.cs:140 | Only Xbox glyphs exist and MatchInput maps buttons by position (MatchInput.cs:70), so PlayStation and Switch controllers show A/B/X/Y prompts that do not match their face buttons. | Detect the Gamepad layout (DualShock / Switch) and add matching glyph sets. |  |
| QA-792 | open | S2 | UI | Input | Gameplay/Match/MatchDirector.cs:496 | Timeout is on three different buttons: D-pad Down in special-teams pre-snap (L475,L495), LT on the play-call screen (L349) and LT in the offense pre-snap hint (PreSnap.cs:224), so a player learns one and the other contexts silently ignore it. | Use one timeout binding everywhere. |  |
| QA-797 | open | S2 | UI | Input | UI/PauseMenu.cs:53 | In free roam Esc is bound to both the gameplay Pause action (FreeRoamGame.cs:70 toggles) and the UI Cancel action (CancelRelay -> Resume); the EventSystem runs first, resumes, then FreeRoamGame sees Pause and pauses again, so Esc cannot close the pause menu (B and Start work). | Ignore the Pause action on the frame the UI consumed Cancel, or remove Esc from the UI cancel binding while paused. |  |
| QA-805 | open | S2 | UI | Settings | UI/TeamSelectPanel.cs:152 | KickOff writes pix.match.home / away / q to PlayerPrefs but nothing ever reads those keys, and CollegeRules, Difficulty and the user's side live only in the static MatchData.Current; after a relaunch all of them (and the last teams) reset, while Weather/Time (L65-66) persist immediately even if you back out without kicking off. | Persist the whole MatchConfig on KICK OFF and restore it on open; apply weather/time only on KICK OFF. |  |
| QA-812 | open | S1 | GFX | Settings | Gameplay/Core/QualityTiers.cs:84 | ApplyScene only ever turns features OFF (shadowDistance = Min, `if (!on) c.active = false`, `if (!tier.SpotShadows) l.shadows = None`); selecting a higher Graphics quality in Settings (or Ultra after Low in the same session) never turns volumetric fog, SSAO, SSR, contact shadows, motion blur or spot shadows back on. | Store the original profile values/light shadow modes once and restore them from the stored baseline when a higher tier is chosen (or reload the scene on change). |  |
| QA-813 | open | S2 | GFX | Settings | Gameplay/Core/QualityTiers.cs:112 | ApplyRenderScale sets `cam.allowDynamicResolution = scale < 0.999f` for every HDRP camera, overriding UpscalerControl.Apply (UpscalerControl.cs:64 sets it true for DLSS/FSR); depending on which runs last at sceneLoaded, and always after changing render scale or quality in Settings, DLSS/FSR at 100 percent render scale is silently switched off. | Make ApplyRenderScale OR with the upscaler mode (read UpscalerControl.Mode) or let a single class own allowDynamicResolution. |  |
| QA-814 | open | S2 | GFX | Settings | UI/SettingsPanel.cs:66 | The Render scale selector only calls QualityTiers.ApplyRenderScale and the Graphics quality selector (L65) only QualityTiers.Select; neither re-runs UpscalerControl.Apply, so an upscaler chosen earlier loses its camera permissions until the next scene load. | Call UpscalerControl.Apply after every render-scale or quality change. |  |
| QA-815 | open | S2 | GFX | Settings | UI/SettingsPanel.cs:67 | The Upscaler selector offers DLSS and FSR on every machine but never tells the player when the choice is unsupported; UpscalerControl.Status ("DLSS not supported on this GPU - using the engine upscaler") is built (UpscalerControl.cs:66) and never displayed. | Show Status next to the selector and grey out unsupported modes (DlssAvailable / Fsr2Available). |  |
| QA-816 | open | S2 | UI | Settings | Gameplay/Core/PixSettings.cs:51 | Apply() only calls Screen.SetResolution when ResolutionWidth > 0, so choosing Windowed (Display mode selector, SettingsPanel.cs:63) without touching Resolution is applied live but forgotten on the next launch (Fullscreen=false saved yet the mode is never re-applied). | Apply fullScreenMode independently of the stored resolution. |  |
| QA-817 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | UI/SettingsPanel.cs:62 | Changing the resolution calls PixSettings.Apply() (which calls Screen.SetResolution) and then Screen.SetResolution again, causing two mode switches/flashes; selecting 3840x2160 on a 1080p monitor is also offered (L119 adds fixed 720p-2160p entries) and never validated. | Call SetResolution once and only list modes the display reports. |  |
| QA-818 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | Gameplay/Core/PixSettings.cs:14 | Fullscreen defaults to 1 whatever the actual window state is, so a first launch in a window (or -screen-fullscreen 0) shows "Display mode: Fullscreen" in Settings while the game is windowed. | Initialise the selector from Screen.fullScreenMode. |  |
| QA-819 | open | S2 | UI | Settings | UI/SettingsPanel.cs:86 | Music volume is consumed only by the main-menu AudioSource (UI/MainMenuController.cs:144,151); no music plays in a match and there is no SFX / commentary / master mixer split (hits, whistles, catches, kicks follow only the Master slider), so "Music volume" does nothing in play and hit/crowd balance cannot be tuned. | Add an SFX slider (and route MatchAudio one-shots through it), or hide Music in match-only contexts. |  |
| QA-821 | open | S2 | UI | Settings | Gameplay/World/LightingController.cs:26 | Apply() and NoteTime() write PixSettings.Lighting; MatchDirector's WeatherSystem calls NoteTime with the resolved time of day, so after a Day / rainy match the Settings > Video > Lighting value (and the free-roam default) silently changes without Save, and the Lighting selector (SettingsPanel.cs:68) duplicates Team Select's Time of day. | Keep a separate "last match time" value and remove the Lighting row from Settings (or make it the default only). |  |
| QA-822 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | Gameplay/Core/PixSettings.cs:45 | Setters never call PlayerPrefs.Save; callers such as MatchDirector.cs:135,346 and FreeRoamGame.cs:73 change DefaultCamera and the weather/time keys without a save, so a crash or force-quit loses them. | Debounce a Save from the setters. |  |
| QA-823 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | Gameplay/Core/PixSettings.cs:28 | LookSensitivity has a getter, setter and consumer (Input/PixInput.cs:75) but no row in the Gameplay settings page, so sensitivity can never be changed. | Add a Look sensitivity slider. |  |
| QA-824 | open | S2 | UI | Settings | Gameplay/Input/PixInput.cs:75 | Invert look Y and Look sensitivity are applied only in PixInput (free roam); the match cameras ignore them (MatchDirector.BuildUserView uses no look input) and the replay free camera reads raw inp.Look (UI path Presentation/Replay/ReplayController.cs:214), so the Gameplay > Invert look Y row does nothing in matches or replays. | Apply the same invert/sensitivity multipliers in ReplayController and MatchInput, or label the rows "Free Roam only". |  |
| QA-825 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | UI/SettingsPanel.cs:72 | The settings pages have no VSync, frame-cap, HUD scale, subtitles/ticker size, or controller remapping rows, and no "restore defaults"; players cannot recover from a bad render-scale/upscaler combination without editing PlayerPrefs. | Add VSync/FPS cap and a Reset to defaults button. |  |
| QA-828 | open | S2 | UI | Input | Gameplay/Input/PixInput.cs:69 | ActiveScheme switches to "Gamepad" whenever Gamepad.current.wasUpdatedThisFrame, which is true every frame for pads that stream reports (wireless / DualSense jitter), and mouse movement never switches to KeyboardMouse (only Keyboard.wasUpdatedThisFrame at L70); glyph/keycap prompts flicker or stick on the wrong device. | Switch on actual control activation (any button/stick beyond a threshold, or mouse delta) and keep the last active device. |  |
| QA-829 | open | S2 | UI | Input | Gameplay/Match/MatchInput.cs:60 | Same device detection in MatchInput: pad.wasUpdatedThisFrame forces UsingGamepad=true every frame for noisy pads, keyboard only wins on the frame a key is held, and the static default GamepadActive=true shows Xbox glyphs to a mouse-only player at the coin toss (no Poll has run yet). | Use the last device that produced a real input event, initialise from connected devices. |  |
| QA-831 | open | S3 | UI | Input | Gameplay/Match/MatchInput.cs:53 | Poll returns the single shared mutable Frame and also advances timers (_navRepeat, _heldFor, _flickCooldown, _prevRsMag) on every call; MatchDirector caches the frame (L770) while ReplayController (L78) and BroadcastDirector.Shows (L28) poll again, so a second call in the same frame can clear NavX/NavY and LookFlick flags and double-count hold times. | Poll once per frame in one place and pass the frame around. |  |
| QA-832 | open | S3 | UI | Input | Gameplay/Match/MatchInput.cs:93 | F is both Btn.B (SPIN / throw button) and Cancel, and Space is both Btn.A and Confirm; on keyboard every B tap in gameplay also raises Cancel and every A tap raises Confirm (callers that read Cancel, e.g. ReplayController.SkipPressed, skip on game buttons). | Read Confirm/Cancel only from the UI map or gate them by context. |  |
| QA-833 | open | S3 | UI | Input | Gameplay/Match/MatchInput.cs:69 | LT / RT are analog thresholds read as holds (0.3); the timeout (LT) in the call screen relies on an edge detect in every consumer (_prevLT at MatchDirector.cs:349 and MatchDirector.PreSnap.cs:56,162), and any new consumer that forgets it will fire a timeout every frame. | Expose LT/RT edges in MatchInputFrame (Down/Up like the face buttons). |  |
| QA-859 | open | S2 | UI | Input | Presentation/Replay/ReplayHud.cs:85 | Replay transport prompts (A PLAY / PAUSE, X SLOW-MO, Y ANGLE, LB, RB, B EXIT and the D-pad / stick row) are Xbox glyph FooterPrompts only, so keyboard players see no keys (Z X C R F Space Q E G T Shift V); the auto-replay "A SKIP REPLAY" (L61) likewise. | Use UiPrompts.ButtonIcon so keycaps appear when MatchInput.GamepadActive is false. |  |
| QA-876 | open | S2 | UI | Input | Presentation/BroadcastDirector.Shows.cs:259 | Halftime/postgame page footers (A NEXT, B SKIP) are Xbox-glyph only, the return page (L384) has no Cancel/Menu handling (B does nothing), and the page skip uses Down[B] or Cancel or Menu so Esc skips the whole show to the end instead of just closing the page. | Use scheme-aware prompts and let B step back one page. |  |
| QA-893 | open | S3 | GFX | Settings | Gameplay/Core/UpscalerControl.cs:56 | With DLSS or FSR 2 active the settings use DLSSUseOptimalSettings / FSR2UseOptimalSettings with a fixed Balanced quality (L56-57), so the Render scale selector has no effect on the internal resolution and there is no Quality / Performance choice for the upscaler. | Add an upscaler quality selector and disable Render scale while an advanced upscaler is on. |  |
| QA-895 | open | S3 | UI | Input | Gameplay/Input/PixInput.cs:40 | ActiveScheme defaults to "Gamepad" and FreeRoamHud draws glyphs until the first key press; a keyboard-only player starts the game looking at Xbox buttons in the legend (also MatchInput.GamepadActive default true). | Initialise the scheme from Gamepad.current != null. |  |
| QA-978 | open | S2 | GFX | Settings | ProjectSettings/QualitySettings.asset (lodBias per level) | lodBias is non-monotonic across quality levels: Very Low 0.3, Low 1, Medium 0.7, High 1, Very High 1.5, Ultra 1. Low draws more detail than Medium and Ultra less than Very High; the uncommitted diff also changed one level from 0.4 to 1. | Make lodBias strictly increasing with quality (e.g. 0.5, 0.7, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0). |  |
| QA-979 | open | S2 | UI | Settings | ProjectSettings/QualitySettings.asset (m_CurrentQuality: 1) | The working copy changed m_CurrentQuality from 5 (Ultra) to 1 (Low) and shadowmaskMode on Low to 1; only Ultra has customRenderPipeline set (fileID 11400000) and m_PerPlatformDefaultQuality Standalone is 5, so the editor session and the Standalone default use different levels and editor screenshots no longer match the shipped quality. | Revert m_CurrentQuality to 5 or document it, and assign the HDRP asset to every level. |  |
| QA-980 | open | S3 | GFX | Settings | ProjectSettings/QualitySettings.asset (skinWeights per level) | skinWeights is 1 (Bone1) on Very Low and 255 (unlimited) on Ultra while PlayerAvatar forces SkinQuality.Bone4 per renderer; the legacy value is inconsistent with the project's assumption and Very Low would skin 22 players with one bone if ever selected. | Set skinWeights to 4 on all levels. |  |
| QA-981 | open | S2 | UI | Settings | ProjectSettings/ProjectSettings.asset (m_BuildTargetGraphicsAPIs: WindowsStandaloneSupport 12000000i, m_Automatic 0) | Only Direct3D12 is allowed on Windows standalone with automatic API off, no D3D11 or Vulkan fallback; GPUs or drivers without D3D12 crash at startup with no message. PatchNotes say DX12 required but the player gives no friendly error. | Add a launch check that shows a message, or keep Vulkan as a secondary API. |  |
| QA-982 | open | S2 | UI | Settings | ProjectSettings/ProjectSettings.asset (runInBackground: 0, visibleInBackground: 1) | runInBackground is off, so the match pauses (and the audio stops, and timers freeze) whenever the window loses focus, e.g. when a controller overlay or the Steam overlay grabs focus; autotests in batch/background also stall. | Set runInBackground to 1 for a game that streams audio and the sim. |  |
| QA-983 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | ProjectSettings/ProjectSettings.asset (scriptingBackend empty, managedStrippingLevel empty, il2cppCodeGeneration empty) | No scripting backend or stripping level is set for Windows standalone, so the build uses the default (Mono) with default stripping; the engine-free 60 Hz sim for 22 agents plus the 30 Hz replay recorder would run faster on IL2CPP, and a managed-stripping level should be tested explicitly. | Decide IL2CPP vs Mono explicitly for Windows and record it in RELEASING.md. |  |
| QA-984 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | ProjectSettings/TimeManager.asset (Fixed Timestep 0.02) vs Docs/ARCHITECTURE.md (Sim section 60 Hz) | Physics runs at 50 Hz while the simulation, replay recorder (30 Hz) and docs assume 60 Hz; ragdoll and ball physics therefore step at a different rate than the sim and the replays, causing judder when ragdoll bodies are blended with the 60 Hz view. | Set fixed timestep to 1/60 or document the decoupling. |  |
| QA-985 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | ProjectSettings/TagManager.asset (tags empty, only builtin layers) | There are no custom layers or tags: players, ragdoll bodies, ball, field and trigger helpers all live on Default with the all-ones layer collision matrix in DynamicsManager.asset, so ragdoll rigs collide with capsules and each other and raycasts need name checks. | Add layers (Player, Ragdoll, Ball, Field, Camera) and configure the collision matrix. |  |
| QA-986 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | ProjectSettings/AudioManager.asset (m_RealVoiceCount 32, m_DSPBufferSize 1024) | Only 32 real voices for 22 players' footsteps and hits, crowd layers, whistle, UI and music; voice stealing mutes SFX in busy plays, and DSP buffer 1024 adds ~23 ms latency to hit sounds. | Raise real voices to 64-128 and use Good latency (256-512). |  |
| QA-987 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | Packages/manifest.json | The manifest keeps unused packages and modules (ai.navigation, timeline, 2d.sprite, 2d.tilemap and modules androidjni, adaptiveperformance, cloth, terrain, terrainphysics, tetgen, vehicles, wind, video, vectorgraphics, xr, unityanalytics, umbra, physics2d, tilemap); grep finds no use in Assets/_Project (Timeline, NavMesh, Terrain, Tilemap, Video, Cloth, Physics2D). The CHANGELOG 0.5.0 says unused template packages were removed, but these remain and enlarge compile/import. | Remove unused packages and modules. |  |
| QA-988 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | ProjectSettings/EditorBuildSettings.asset and Assets/_Project/Scenes | The build ships Stadium_Pro.unity (v0.1 Free Roam sandbox) and Match_Pro.unity, two ~26,000-line scenes that duplicate the whole stadium (253 objects, 228 crowd chunk renderers each) plus MainMenu; the release carries both copies. | Share the stadium via a prefab/additive scene or drop Free Roam from release builds. |  |
| QA-991 | open | S3 | GFX | Settings | Assets/_Project/Input/PixControls.inputactions and Assets/_Project/Scripts/Gameplay/Match/MatchInput.cs:59-93 | PixControls.inputactions holds only the v0.1 Free Roam "Gameplay" map; the match polls Gamepad.current and Keyboard.current directly with hard-coded keys, so there is no rebinding, no UI action map, and the two keyboard layouts conflict: L is ToggleLighting in PixControls but look-right in MatchInput, T is dpad-down in the match, C/R/F/G mean different things per context. | Move the match to an InputActionAsset with UI/Match/Replay maps and expose rebinding. |  |
| QA-992 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | Assets/_Project/Input/PixControls.inputactions (Look binding Mouse delta scaleVector2 0.05) | Mouse look is bound with a fixed 0.05 scale, no sensitivity setting and no cursor lock handling; keyboard players have no way to use the right-stick moves (flick juke, hold-down protect) except IJKL, which share keys with navigation. | Add mouse sensitivity/cursor settings or document keyboard equivalents for stick gestures. |  |
| QA-993 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Gameplay/Match/MatchInput.cs:87-93 | Keyboard Cancel maps to F and Backspace while F is also the Spin button (East) and Space is both Confirm and Hurdle (A); where a menu and a play input overlap one key press fires two actions. | Use context-specific bindings and document them. |  |
| QA-1040 | open | S3 | GFX | Settings | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Build/SceneBuilder.cs:63-65 | The editor volume profiles are generated with contact-shadow length 0.25 m, SSR minSmoothness 0.92, SSAO radius 0.7 and a single ACES tonemapper with no LUT; these values are repeated in code (CommonPost) and in the .asset files and drift apart when one is edited by hand (the uncommitted diff already re-serialised 2900 lines in the three volume assets). | Make the .asset files the single source and stop regenerating them in BuildAll. |  |
| QA-1048 | open | S3 | UI | Settings | Assets/Resources/BillingMode.json | A leftover {"androidStore":"GooglePlay"} file from the removed com.unity.purchasing package still sits in Resources (included in every build) although CHANGELOG 0.5.0 says the purchasing/analytics packages were removed. | Delete the file. |  |

## 9. Audio (8)

| ID | Status | Sev | Track | Category | Where | Issue | Fix | Verified by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| QA-767 | open | S3 | UI | Audio | Art/PixRuntimeAssets.cs:26 | uiClick, uiMove and sfxUi are imported but no code ever plays them: every menu (navigate, select, back) is silent. | Add a UiAudio helper that plays uiMove on selection change and uiClick on submit. |  |
| QA-801 | open | S3 | UI | Audio | UI/MainMenuController.cs:152 | NextTrack fires whenever `!_music.isPlaying`; when the window loses focus or the audio device resets isPlaying becomes false and a track is skipped, and tracks hard-cut with no fade between menu and scene load. | Track elapsed time instead of isPlaying and fade out on scene change. |  |
| QA-878 | open | S3 | CAM | Audio | Presentation/World/CrowdReaction.cs:124 | CrowdReaction.Update uses unscaledDeltaTime, so the crowd keeps waving and 70 camera flashes keep popping while the game is paused (pause menu open) and during manual replay. | Use scaled time or freeze while GameSession.Paused. |  |
| QA-880 | open | S3 | UI | Audio | Gameplay/Match/MatchAudio.cs:51 | Hit() plays the crack on a single AudioSource with Play() (a rapid second tackle cuts the first) and Catch / Kick / BlockHit / Hit share one `_sfx` source whose `pitch` is set per call, so a new sound re-pitches sounds that are still playing. | Use PlayOneShot with a small pool of sources per event type. |  |
| QA-881 | open | S3 | UI | Audio | Gameplay/Match/MatchAudio.cs:27 | All match audio is 2D (spatialBlend 0, no pan), so hits and kicks never pan or attenuate with the camera, and AudioListener.pause is forced false (Core/GameSession.cs:23) so crowd roar and queued hits keep sounding while the pause menu is open. | Add a stereo pan from the contact position and pause SFX on pause. |  |
| QA-882 | open | S3 | UI | Audio | Gameplay/Match/MatchAudio.cs:82 | Quiet() sets _bedTarget=0.3 but Update pulls it back to the 0.38 baseline at 0.08/s, so the crowd "quiet" after a turnover lasts about one second. | Hold the quiet target for a few seconds. |  |
| QA-1041 | open | S3 | UI | Audio | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Build/RuntimeAssetsBuilder.cs:164-172 | Audio keys are hard-coded with date suffixes (music_pp_gates_of_glory_9_10_25, music_pp_catch_up_to_you_8_31_25 ...) and a single footstep_grass clip is used for every surface although footstep_wet, footstep_snow and the weather system exist; if lib is null the whole audio block is skipped without any warning and a silent build results. | Reference tracks by a stable id, select footstep by surface/weather, and error out when the library is missing. |  |
| QA-1042 | open | S3 | UI | Audio | Assets/_Project/Scripts/Editor/Assets/LicensedImportRules.cs:93-100 | SFX are imported DecompressOnLoad with ADPCM and PreserveSampleRate (no force-to-mono for 3D one-shots) and ambience as CompressedInMemory Vorbis 0.5; the 16 music WAVs (57-68 MB each, 1.1 GB) are Streaming Vorbis 0.6, but preloadAudioData is set true for everything except /Audio/Music, so ~46 MB of SFX decompress at scene load and long crowd loops stay in memory. | Force mono for positional SFX, use CompressedInMemory for ambience loops and a smaller sample rate for UI. |  |
